Result: 在LiC配对视图协议和确定性验证器下，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的SHARDED准确率从52.8提升至66.1，SHARDED/FULL比率从66.5%提升至84.1%，FULL准确率仅下降2.3个点。

Conclusion: 自我污染是LiC差距中可训练的成分，MAIGO方法无需额外验证器或推理时辅助即可有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Large language models often solve tasks from a fully specified prompt but degrade when the same requirements unfold over multiple turns, known as the lost-in-conversation (LiC) gap. We trace part of this degradation to self-contamination: intermediate assistant replies enter later context and carry early deviations forward. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose MAIGO, an on-policy self-distillation method that reduces this contamination using history-cleaned references from the model's own policy. For middle turns, MAIGO removes prior assistant replies while preserving the user-visible sharded prefix; for answer turns, it distills from paired full-view references conditioned on the completed user-side dialogue. A reliability weight downweights middle-turn samples that disagree with the clean reference. MAIGO requires no verifier rewards, state labels, or inference-time scaffolding. Under the LiC paired-view protocol with deterministic verifiers, MAIGO improves Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct SHARDED accuracy from 52.8 to 66.1 and the SHARDED/FULL ratio from 66.5% to 84.1%, while keeping FULL accuracy within 2.3 points. These results show that self-contamination is a trainable component of the LiC gap.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Binary: Speech Representations Across the Cognitive Score Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27189)
*Serli Kopar,Roshan Prakash Rane,Christian Mychajliw,Lydia Federmann,Gerhard Eschweiler,Daniela Berg,Sam Gijsen,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用5,754份德语神经心理学评估录音，分析了轻度认知障碍中语音表征与认知评估层次结构的关系。结果显示，自监督学习嵌入在低层次任务中优于手工声学特征，但在MCI分类中趋势反转；任务自由度影响表征性能随层次变化的模式：高自由度任务呈现"专家型"表征（性能随层次升高而稀释），高结构化任务呈现"通才型"表征（性能随层次升高而提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语音表征与认知评估层次结构之间的关系，理解不同层次（任务、领域、全局）的语音特征对轻度认知障碍分类的有效性。

Method: 使用5,754份德语神经心理学评估录音，评估六项认知任务在三个评分层次（任务级、领域级、全局级）的表现，比较手工声学特征与自监督学习（SSL）嵌入的性能。

Result: SSL表征在低层次任务中优于手工特征，但MCI分类中手工特征表现更好；任务自由度影响性能：高自由度任务随层次升高性能下降（专家型），高结构化任务随层次升高性能提升（通才型）。

Conclusion: 任务约束与评估层次之间存在联系，不同任务类型影响自动化临床语音分析中表征性能随层次变化的方向。

Abstract: This study examines the relationship between speech representations and the hierarchical structure of cognitive assessment in mild cognitive impairment. Utilizing 5,754 German neuropsychological assessment recordings, we evaluate six cognitive tasks across three score levels: task, domain, and global levels. We compare hand-crafted acoustic features with self-supervised learning (SSL) embeddings. Results show that although SSL representations generally outperform hand-crafted features at lower levels, this trend reverses for MCI classification. Furthermore, task-specific constraints influence performance: tasks with greater response freedom exhibit performance dilution as hierarchical levels increase, suggesting ``specialist'' representations, whereas the performance of highly structured tasks increases toward higher levels, suggesting ``generalist'' representations. These findings show links between task constraints and assessment hierarchy in automated clinical speech analysis.

</details>


### [79] [Learning When to Think While Listening in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27190)
*Zhiyuan Song,Weici Zhao,Yang Xiao,Suhao Yu,Cheng Zhu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大型音频-语言模型（LALM）的可学习的“等待-思考-回答”控制框架，通过多奖励强化学习（DAPO）优化推理时机，在合成和真实语音问答基准上提升了准确率并降低了响应延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流式语音交互中，推理质量与响应速度之间存在根本性矛盾：过早回答可能因证据不足而出错，过晚回答则导致用户可见的延迟。现有LALM缺乏在部分音频输入下动态决策“何时等待、何时输出中间推理、何时最终回答”的机制。

Method: 基于Qwen2.5-Omni-7B，构建对齐的等待-思考-回答轨迹数据，通过SFT训练控制器，再采用解耦裁剪与动态采样策略优化（DAPO）进行强化学习。奖励函数综合了答案正确性、动作有效性、更新时机、延迟同步、推理质量和链一致性六项指标，优化整个轨迹而非仅最终答案。

Result: 在六任务合成SRQA基准上，六奖励DAPO控制器将行加权准确率从67.6%提升至70.3%，同时将端点后最终思考长度减少14%。在186条真实录音的Real Audio Bench上，SFT取得最强准确率，六奖励DAPO是唯一最终思考长度低于基线的学习变体。

Conclusion: 流式LALM应当学习在音频流中何时将中间推理显式化，这种动态的等待-思考-回答控制能在保证准确率的同时有效降低响应延迟。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have made real-time, streaming spoken interaction increasingly practical. In this setting, reasoning quality and responsiveness are tightly coupled: delaying reasoning until the speech endpoint can improve answer quality but moves deliberation into user-visible response delay, while answering too early risks committing before decisive evidence arrives. We introduce a learnable wait-think-answer control formulation for LALMs. Motivated by the incremental nature of human conversation, the controller decides under partial audio evidence when to wait, when to externalize a compact reasoning update, and when to answer. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the base model, we construct aligned wait-think-answer traces from spoken reasoning data, train the controller with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and then apply Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO). The reward combines answer correctness, action validity, update timing, latency synchronization, reasoning quality, and chain consistency, optimizing the complete wait-think-answer trajectory and not the final answer alone. On a six-task synthetic spoken reasoning question answering (SRQA) benchmark, the six-reward DAPO controller improves the row-weighted accuracy from 67.6% to 70.3% while reducing post-endpoint final-think length by 14% under the same Qwen deployment harness. On a 186-item human-recorded Real Audio Bench, a transfer check beyond text-to-speech (TTS)-rendered speech, the controller family remains functional: SFT achieves the strongest accuracy, while the six-reward DAPO controller is the only learned variant whose final-think length falls below the base. These results suggest that a streaming model should learn when to make intermediate reasoning explicit during the audio stream.

</details>


### [80] [EpiCurveBench: Evaluating VLMs on Epidemic Curve Digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27195)
*Thomas Berkane,Maimuna S. Majumder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有图表数据提取评估中忽略时间序列结构和指标过于粗糙的问题，本文提出了EpiCurveBench基准（1000张真实流行病曲线图）和EpiCurveSimilarity (ECS)评估指标（基于动态规划，容忍局部时间偏移并以比例惩罚）。最强模型仅达52.3% ECS，且ECS能在通用VLM间拉开25个百分点的差距（而传统指标仅5点）。ECS与四种下游流行病统计量相关性比DTW强1.5-3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表数据提取评估存在两大缺陷：1) 前沿VLM在ChartQA等基准上已达89%以上，天花板效应明显；2) 现有评估指标将提取点视为无序键值对，忽略了时间序列的时序结构，且对微小对齐偏移进行灾难性惩罚。这限制了对真实世界时间序列图表提取能力的准确评估。

Method: 1) 构建EpiCurveBench：1,000张来自多种公共卫生来源的真实流行病曲线图像；2) 提出ECS指标：基于动态规划对齐预测序列与真实序列，容忍局部时间偏移和间隙但按比例惩罚；3) 评估六种方法（三个前沿闭源VLM、一个开源VLM、两个专用图表提取系统）；4) 与四种下游流行病学统计量（总计数、峰值时间、峰值幅度、增长速率保真度）做相关性验证。

Result: 最强模型仅达52.3% ECS；ECS将四种通用VLM在25个百分点的范围内拉开差距，而传统RMS/SCRM指标仅压缩在5点范围内；ECS与四种下游统计量的相关性比DTW强1.5-3.6倍。

Conclusion: EpiCurveBench和ECS有效弥补了现有图表提取评估的时序结构缺失问题，不仅适用于流行病曲线这一高影响力的公共卫生应用（解锁被困在已发表图表中的数十年疫情数据），也直接适用于任何结构化时间序列图表提取场景。

Abstract: Chart-to-data extraction with vision-language models (VLMs) is increasingly evaluated on benchmarks that show diminishing headroom (frontier VLMs exceed 89% on ChartQA) and with metrics that treat extracted points as unordered key-value pairs, ignoring the temporal structure of time series and penalizing small alignment shifts as catastrophic failures. We address both gaps with EpiCurveBench, a benchmark of 1,000 real-world epidemic curve images curated from diverse public-health sources, and EpiCurveSimilarity (ECS), an evaluation metric that aligns predicted and ground-truth series via dynamic programming, tolerating local temporal shifts and gaps while penalizing them proportionally. Evaluating six methods--three frontier closed VLMs, one open VLM, and two specialized chart-extraction systems--we find the strongest model reaches only 52.3% ECS, and that ECS spreads the four general-purpose VLMs over a 25-point range where key-value metrics (RMS, SCRM) compress them into a 5-point band. We further validate ECS against four downstream epidemiological summary statistics, finding that higher ECS predicts smaller errors in total counts, peak timing, and peak magnitude, and higher growth-rate fidelity; across all four, ECS correlates 1.5--3.6 times more strongly than Dynamic Time Warping, which lacks a gap penalty and therefore cannot distinguish a truncated prediction from a temporally faithful one. EpiCurveBench targets a high-impact public-health application--unlocking decades of outbreak data trapped in published figures--but the benchmark and metric apply directly to any structured time-series chart-extraction setting.

</details>


### [81] [The Coverage Illusion: From Pre-retrieval Routing Failure to Post-retrieval Cascades in a Production RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27220)
*Zafar Hussain,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示了RAG流水线中查询增强方法（如HyDE和查询扩展）存在一个“覆盖幻觉”（Coverage Illusion）现象：基于合成查询的评估表明90%以上查询需要LLM增强，但在实际生产流量中仅27.8%的真实用户查询需要LLM增强。作者提出一种免训练的后检索级联策略，按最便宜优先顺序执行工作流，仅在无结果时升级到LLM增强，在丹麦国家百科全书的案例中取得了+0.140综合得分提升、31.8%延迟降低，且72.2%的真实查询无需LLM增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG流水线中普遍对每个查询都应用HyDE和查询扩展等增强方法，导致大量LLM推理成本和端到端延迟增加，但这些开销在实际生产流量中的必要性尚未被充分探究。

Method: 基于丹麦国家百科全书的案例研究，评估了20,000个查询-工作流对的5种检索工作流，对比合成查询与真实生产流量；提出后检索级联策略，按成本升序执行检索工作流，仅在无返回结果时升级到LLM增强。

Result: 合成查询显示90%以上需要LLM增强，但生产流量中仅27.8%需要；提出的后检索级联策略相比Always-HyDE提升了+0.140综合得分，降低了31.8%延迟，72.2%的真实查询无需LLM增强。

Conclusion: 合成查询与真实查询分布之间存在结构性不匹配导致了覆盖幻觉；由于对LLM增强的需求只有在搜索索引后才能揭示，前检索路由无法解决这一差距；后检索级联策略无需训练开销或辅助服务设施即可有效降低延迟和成本。

Abstract: In modern RAG pipelines, query augmentation methods such as HyDE and query expansion are applied to every query, resulting in substantial LLM inference costs and increased end-to-end latency. The empirical justification for this overhead in real production traffic remains largely unexplored. We present a case study of the Danish National Encyclopedia, evaluating five retrieval workflows over 20,000 query-workflow pairs from production traffic and synthetic conditions. In this system, synthetic queries suggest that LLM augmentation is needed for over 90% of queries to achieve high retrieval coverage. However, under our production deferral policy, only 27.8% of real user queries need LLM augmentation. We call this gap the Coverage Illusion and attribute it to a structural mismatch between synthetic and real query distributions. Pre-retrieval routing cannot resolve this gap, as the need for LLM augmentation is only revealed after searching the index, a result confirmed by our evaluation of four machine learning paradigms. The coverage gap, undetectable from the query alone, motivates a post-retrieval cascade that runs workflows in cheapest-first order and escalates to LLM augmentation only when a step returns no documents. Operating entirely without training overhead or secondary serving infrastructure, the cascade improves quality by +0.140 Composite Overall points over Always-HyDE, reduces latency by 31.8%, and serves 72.2% of real user queries without LLM augmentation.

</details>


### [82] [Temporal Simultaneity Predicts Annotation Quality in Sentiment Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27239)
*Idris Abdulmumin,Mokgadi Penelope Matloga,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Botshelo Kondowe,Letlhogonolo Mohleleng,Hareaipha Nkopo Letsoalo,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Vukosi Marivate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究Setswana情感数据集（3,565条推文）标注质量随时间下降的问题。尽管总体Kappa系数为0.76（优秀），但逐批次Kappa下降超32点。主要发现：标签混淆集中在消极/中性边界；两名标注者出现"自动驾驶"漂移；时间同步性是Kappa的主要预测因子（一分钟内标注Kappa=0.98，相隔一天以上仅0.65）。微调多语言编码器相比预训练基线提升29-43 macro-F1，GPT-5少样本学习最优（62.2 macro-F1）。已开源数据集、时间戳和分析代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标注质量在长期、小规模标注池项目中难以维持的问题，特别是低资源非洲语言（Setswana）情感数据集的标注一致性随时间下降的原因。

Method: 使用3位母语标注者对3,565条Setswana推文分8批次进行标注；采用Randolph's free-marginal Kappa评估一致性；通过六项针对性分析探究IAA下降原因（标签混淆、标注者漂移、时间同步性、速度、语言特征）；微调三种开源多语言编码器及GPT-5、Gemini进行三分类情感分类。

Result: 总体Kappa=0.76（优秀），但逐批次Kappa下降超32点。标签混淆集中于消极/中性边界；两名标注者出现run-length drift（自动驾驶模式）；时间同步性是Kappa最强预测因子（一分钟内Kappa=0.98，一天以上仅0.65）。微调提升29-43 macro-F1，GPT-5少样本学习达62.2 macro-F1。

Conclusion: 标注一致性随时间显著下降主要由时间不同步和标注者漂移导致，而非语言特征或标注速度。建议非洲语言NLP资源构建中应引入时间戳记录以支持质量审计。开源数据和分析代码可促进可复现的质量审计。

Abstract: Annotation quality is difficult to sustain when campaigns span weeks or months with small annotator pools. We present a Setswana sentiment dataset of 3,565 tweets annotated by three native-speaker annotators across eight batches and examine why inter-annotator agreement (IAA) declines over time. Despite an aggregate Randolph's free-marginal Kappa of $κ= 0.76$, "excellent," per-batch $κ$ falls by more than 32 points across the annotation task. Through six targeted analyses, we find that (i) label confusion concentrates on the negative/neutral boundary, (ii) two annotators show run-length drift consistent with autopilot labeling, and (iii) the dominant predictor of $κ$ is temporal simultaneity: tweets labeled within one minute achieve $κ= 0.98$, while those labeled more than a day apart reach only $κ= 0.65$. Annotation speed and tweet-level linguistic features show no meaningful association with $κ$. We benchmark three open multilingual encoders and proprietary models (GPT-5 and Gemini) on three-class sentiment classification; fine-tuning yields gains of 29 to 43 macro-F1 points over pretrained baselines, with GPT-5 few-shot leading overall (62.2 macro-F1). We release the dataset, per-annotation timestamps, and analysis code to support reproducible quality auditing for future African language NLP resources.

</details>


### [83] [Pair-In, Pair-Out: Latent Multi-Token Prediction for Efficient LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27255)
*Wenhui Tan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Siqi Fan,Xiusheng Huang,Liujie Zhang,Ruihua Song,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PIPO 通过将输入压缩与输出多token预测统一为镜像操作，并利用轻量置信度头消除验证开销，实现了最高2.64倍首token延迟和2.07倍逐token延迟加速，同时提升pass@4最多7.15个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法分别处理输入侧（潜在压缩）和输出侧（投机解码/多token预测），但两者独立发展；且输出侧方法需昂贵的验证器来校验MTP产出的不可靠草稿token，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)，将输入压缩器（合并两个输入token为一个潜在表示）与MTP头（展开一个隐藏状态为一个额外输出token）视为镜像操作统一处理；训练轻量置信度头决定是否接受草稿token，利用On-Policy Distillation (OPD)自然匹配投机解码的拒绝采样标准，无需额外验证开销。

Result: 在AIME 2025、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench v6、LongBench v2上，以Qwen3.5-4B和9B为骨干，PIPO的pass@4比常规解码提升最多+7.15点，首token延迟加速最高2.64倍，逐token延迟加速最高2.07倍。

Conclusion: PIPO通过统一输入压缩与输出预测、消除验证开销，在推理效率与生成质量上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning has made autoregressive decoding the dominant inference cost of modern large language models. Existing methods target either the input side (latent compression) or the output side (speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, MTP), but the two lines of work have been pursued independently. Moreover, output-side methods must incur an expensive verifier pass to validate the unreliable draft tokens predicted by MTP. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)}, which unifies both sides by viewing a latent compressor and an MTP head as mirror-image operations: the compressor folds two input tokens into one latent representation, while the MTP head unfolds one hidden state into one additional output token. To remove the verifier cost without sacrificing reliability, PIPO trains a lightweight confidence head that decides whether draft tokens should be accepted. We observe that On-Policy Distillation (OPD) naturally matches the rejection-sampling criterion of speculative decoding, so the confidence head can be trained alongside OPD with negligible extra cost. Experiments on AIME 2025, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench v6, and LongBench v2 with Qwen3.5-4B and 9B backbones show that PIPO improves pass@4 over regular decoding by up to $+7.15$ points, while delivering up to $2.64\times$ first-token-latency and $2.07\times$ per-token-latency speedups.

</details>


### [84] [Lost in Sampling: Assessing Lexical Reachability in LLMs via the Word Coverage Score (WCS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27268)
*Samer Awad,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Tairan Fu,Javier Coronado-Blázquez,Pedro Reviriego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出词汇覆盖率评分(WCS)来量化标准采样过滤器（Top-p、Top-k、Min-p）如何数学性地修剪掉低频率、高信息量的人类词汇，揭示行业默认采样参数作为一种无意识的审查机制，将人类表达平滑为同质化话语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs生成的文本重复且同质化，先前研究关注模型知识和训练数据，但本文探究解码机制本身对语言多样性的抑制作用。

Method: 提出Word Coverage Score (WCS)指标，衡量在给定采样参数下，低频率、高信息量的人类词汇在标准采样过滤器（Top-p、Top-k、Min-p）中的数学存活率。通过审计开源模型在人类撰写的语料片段上的表现，识别哪些符合语境的词汇选择因解码器而变得不可达。

Result: 发现行业标准的采样默认值实际上充当了无意识的审查机制，消除了人类表达的独特纹理，导致同质化话语。

Conclusion: WCS为优化文本连贯性与词汇丰富性之间的权衡提供了严谨框架，是一个保留生成模型中人类语言多样性的诊断工具。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) are often criticized for producing repetitive and homogeneous text, despite possessing vast latent vocabularies. While previous research has focused on model knowledge and training data, we investigate the role of decoding mechanics in suppressing linguistic diversity. We introduce the Word Coverage Score (WCS), a metric that quantifies the extent to which contextually appropriate human vocabulary is mathematically pruned by standard sampling filters (e.g., Top-$p$, Top-$k$, and Min-$p$). Rather than assessing static knowledge, the WCS measures the lexical survival rate of low-frequency, high-information human words as a function of sampling parameters. By auditing open-weight models on human-authored corpus fragments, we identify which logical lexical choices are rendered unreachable by the decoder, even when they reside within the probability space. Our results provide quantitative evidence that industry-standard sampling defaults act as unintended censorship mechanisms, smoothing the unique textures of human expression into a homogenized discourse. The WCS offers a rigorous framework for optimizing the trade-off between text coherence and lexical richness, providing a diagnostic tool for preserving the diversity of human language in generative models.

</details>


### [85] [It's Not Always Sycophancy: Measuring LLM Conformity as a Function of Epistemic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27288)
*Kevin H. Guo,Chao Yan,Avinash Baidya,Katherine Brown,Xiang Gao,Juming Xiong,Zhijun Yin,Bradley A. Malin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSE框架，用于解耦大模型顺从行为的两种驱动机制：谄媚性顺从（即使模型对初始回答完全确定也会因用户压力而改变）和不确定性驱动的顺从（模型的不确定性越高，顺从倾向越强）。实验表明这两种顺从行为均随用户专业感知和用户建议合理性增加而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将大模型在用户反驳时放弃初始立场的现象主要归因于RLHF训练导致的谄媚（sycophancy），但作者假设这种顺从行为也受到模型推理时 epistemic uncertainty（认知不确定性）的驱动。现有方法无法区分这两种机制。

Method: 提出MUSE（两阶段评估框架）：第一阶段评估模型对某个查询的认知不确定性，第二阶段观察模型在后续轮次中面对用户反驳时的顺从倾向。通过此框架解耦谄媚性顺从和不确定性驱动的顺从，并进行消融实验分析用户专业感知和用户建议合理性因素的影响。

Result: 1) 顺从行为确实由两种因素共同驱动：谄媚性顺从和不确定性驱动的顺从。2) 两种顺从行为均随用户感知专业度和用户建议合理性的提升而增强。3) 谄媚性顺从与RLHF对齐相关，而不确定性驱动的顺从与训练语料分布相关。

Conclusion: MUSE框架能够区分由对齐导致的谄媚和由训练语料导致的不确定性两种机制，从而为更精准的干预策略（如针对性处理谄媚或减少模型不确定性）提供指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to abandon their initial stance to conform to user pushback. While prior research largely attributes this behavior to sycophancy learned during reinforcement learning from human feedback, we hypothesize that conformity is also driven by a model's epistemic uncertainty at inference time. In this paper, we introduce MUSE, a two-stage evaluation framework to disentangle the mechanisms driving LLM conformity. Specifically, MUSE maps a model's epistemic uncertainty in responding to a query against its likelihood to yield to user pushback in a subsequent turn. We demonstrate that the mechanisms driving conformity extend beyond sycophancy alone. Specifically, we characterize two distinct factors that jointly drive conformity: sycophantic conformity, where a model aligns with user pushback even with absolute certainty in its initial response, and uncertainty-driven conformity, where a model's likelihood for conformity increases alongside its uncertainty. Furthermore, we conduct ablation studies to demonstrate that both sycophantic conformity and uncertainty-driven conformity grow with 1) the LLM's perceived expertise of the user and 2) the plausibility of the user's suggestions. More broadly, MUSE informs more targeted intervention strategies by distinguishing alignment-induced sycophancy and training-corpora-driven uncertainty.

</details>


### [86] [Separating Semantic Competition from Context Length in RAG Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27294)
*Vyzantinos Repantis,Ameya Gawde,Harshvardhan Singh,Rohit Alekar,Cien Zhang,Svetlana Karslioglu,Akash Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种针对RAG系统中阅读器的匹配对照实验协议，通过替换高竞争性段落为低竞争性段落，分离出段落间竞争效应与上下文长度效应，证明竞争而非单纯长度是导致阅读器性能下降的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统即使检索到正确段落也可能回答错误，但原因不明——究竟是上下文变长导致，还是其他检索段落与正确段落形成了竞争关系？现有研究未能分离这两种效应。

Method: 提出匹配对照协议（matched-control protocol）：固定段落数量和长度不变，仅将高竞争性段落替换为低竞争性的真实段落。在SQuAD数据集上对Phi-2和Qwen2.5-1.5B两个紧凑开源模型进行实验，并绘制保留曲线（retention curves），使用右删失半衰期（right-censored half-life）作为总结指标。

Result: 替换竞争段落可部分恢复性能：Phi-2提升+6.0 EM、+7.0 答案包含率、+0.057 F1；Qwen2.5-1.5B提升+4.5 EM、+9.0 答案包含率、+0.068 F1。竞争效应对F1和答案包含率的改善效果比精确匹配（EM）更明显，且随片段长度变化。

Conclusion: 匹配对照协议成功分离出段落间竞争效应，证明该效应独立于上下文长度，是导致RAG阅读器性能下降的重要原因。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can respond incorrectly even when the correct passage was retrieved. The model must still read the retrieved passages and identify which one contains the answer among others that look relevant. This passage-reading model is called the reader. Does it fail simply because the context is longer or because the other passages genuinely compete with the correct one? We introduce and demonstrate a matched-control protocol for RAG reading: we keep the number and length of passages fixed, but replace hard competitors with less competitive real passages. We apply this control across two compact open models on SQuAD. This replacement partially restores performance, with the strongest effects on F1 and answer inclusion. For Phi-2, this recovers +6.0 EM points, +7.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.057 F1. For Qwen2.5-1.5B, it recovers +4.5 EM points, +9.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.068 F1. To track how performance changes as competitors accumulate, we also report retention curves and summarize them with a right-censored half-life when the curves do not cross half-retention. Together, these results show the protocol isolates a competition effect distinct from context length, though the effect is clearer for F1 and answer inclusion than for exact match, and also varies with snippet length.

</details>


### [87] [Probing Cultural Awareness in LLMs: A Case Study of Cross-Culture Aesthetic Stylistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27296)
*Jiashuo Wang,Fenggang Yu,Jian Wang,Chak Tou Leong,Xiaoyu Shen,Chunpu Xu,Jiawen Duan,Wenjie Li,Johan F. Hoorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了C4STYLI基准来评估LLM在不同文化背景下的文体风格掌握能力，发现LLM在风格识别上与人类存在差异，且识别能力依赖于表层语言信息而非深层风格结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多元文化背景下部署时，其掌握美学文体风格（即通过语言策略引发文化共鸣）的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建C4STYLI基准，包含香港和中国大陆高度风格化的翻译电影片名和广告标语；通过行为识别和生成能力两个视角评估LLM；使用逻辑回归探针进行结构性消融实验。

Result: LLM在风格识别上与人类存在差异，且识别能力因文本领域而异；风格识别与生成性能不一致；在香港设定下，LLM的风格识别主要依赖表层语言信息而非风格结构。

Conclusion: 当前LLM对特定文化（如香港）的深层风格结构敏感度有限，风格能力存在局限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in diverse cultural contexts, yet their ability to master aesthetic stylistics, i.e., the strategic use of language to evoke cultural resonance, remains underexplored. We curate C4STYLI, a benchmark of highly stylized translated movie titles and advertising slogans from Hong Kong and the Chinese Mainland, to evaluate LLMs via the lens of behavioral recognition and productive competence. Extensive evaluations show that LLMs differ from humans in stylistic recognition, and this recognition ability varies across text domains. In addition, stylistic recognition and generation performance in LLMs are not consistently aligned. To further examine whether LLMs genuinely capture stylistic information in stylistic recognition, we conduct structural ablation with logistic regression probes. We find that, in the Hong Kong setting, stylistic recognition in LLMs relies primarily on surface-level linguistic information rather than stylistic structure. This suggests limited sensitivity to Hong Kong-specific stylistic structure.

</details>


### [88] [Self-Ensembling Vision-Language Models for Chart Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27298)
*Thomas Berkane,Qianyi Wang,Maimuna S. Majumder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种VLM自集成方法，通过多次采样和聚合图表表格输出，提升图表到表格的提取精度，尤其适用于数据点密集和风格多样的图表。同时构建了更复杂的WB-ChartExtract基准，在多个基准上取得了最高23%的相对改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表中的定量数据常以图像形式存在，难以复用和分析。现有VLM方法在数据点多或风格变化大的图表上性能不足，亟需更准确且可靠的图表到表格提取方法。

Method: 提出VLM自集成方法：对同一图表图像多次采样多个表格输出，对齐候选表格后对每个单元格的数值取中位数作为共识结果；引入收敛检测机制在聚合稳定后停止采样，并基于样本间的离散程度估计不确定性，帮助用户评估提取可靠性。

Result: 在ChartQA和WB-ChartExtract两个基准上，集成方法优于单次VLM输出；在更复杂（数据点平均多7倍）的WB-ChartExtract上取得最高23%的相对改进。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解锁了图表图像中的表格数据，支持下游分析与复用，为图表提取任务提供了更准确且可量化不确定性的解决方案。

Abstract: Charts effectively convey quantitative information, but the underlying data are often locked in image form, hindering reuse and analysis. Manually digitizing charts is time-consuming and error-prone, motivating automatic chart-to-table extraction. Recent approaches use specialized vision-language models (VLMs), yet performance still lags on charts with many datapoints or substantial stylistic variation. We propose a VLM self-ensembling method that repeatedly samples multiple tabular outputs from the same VLM for a fixed chart image and aggregates them at the level of individual table cells. We align candidate tables and take per-cell medians over numerical values to produce a more accurate consensus table. Our method also includes convergence detection to stop sampling once the aggregated table stabilizes, and uncertainty estimation based on dispersion across samples to help users assess extraction reliability. Because existing chart extraction benchmarks contain relatively simple plots with limited room for improvement, we introduce WB-ChartExtract, a new benchmark built from World Bank data with more complex and stylistically diverse charts; on average, its charts contain 7 times more datapoints than those in the ChartQA benchmark. Across both ChartQA and WB-ChartExtract, our approach improves extraction accuracy over single-pass VLM outputs, yielding up to 23% relative improvement on WB-ChartExtract after ensembling. More broadly, our method helps unlock tabular data previously siloed in chart images, enabling downstream analysis and reuse.

</details>


### [89] [Chartographer: Counterfactual Chart Generation for Evaluating Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27311)
*Yifan Jiang,Dae Yon Hwang,Jesse C. Cresswell,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出反事实图表框架Chartographer，通过逆向工程将图表转为可执行代码，生成种子控制的反事实变体，用于严格评估VLM的视觉推理能力。实验发现VLM在原始图表正确回答后，面对反事实变体时常常无法泛化，尤其当需要新的视觉推理路径时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表问答基准中，模型常依赖捷径或背景知识而非真正的视觉推理来回答问题。为了严格评估视觉推理能力，需要一种方法排除模型利用捷径的可能性。

Method: 提出Chartographer框架，包含以下步骤：(1) 逆向工程将图表转为可执行代码；(2) 验证重建保真度；(3) 生成种子控制的反事实变体（保持图表-问题任务固定，但底层图表数据及答案变化）；(4) 从可执行的QA逻辑中推导新答案。将框架应用于现有图表QA数据集，评估商业和开源VLM。

Result: 反事实图表揭示了被单一图表性能掩盖的失败模式：VLM在正确回答原始图表后，面对反事实变体时常常无法泛化。失败在需要全新视觉推理路径的更新图表中最常见。

Conclusion: 现有VLM的视觉推理能力被高估，反事实图表是一种更严格的评估方法，能暴露模型在视觉推理上的真实缺陷。

Abstract: Chart question-answering (QA) benchmarks aim to pose questions that require visual reasoning to correctly answer, but models can often reach solutions through shortcuts or prior familiarity with a chart based on their own background knowledge. To strictly evaluate visual reasoning, we propose counterfactual charts where the chart-question task remains fixed, but underlying chart and the corresponding answer are varied. We introduce Chartographer, a framework to reverse engineer charts into executable code, validate reconstruction fidelity, generate seed-controlled counterfactual variants, and derive new answers from executable QA logic. We apply this framework to existing chart QA datasets and evaluate proprietary and open-source vision-language models (VLMs), measuring variation sensitivity and generalizability. Counterfactual charts reveal failures hidden by single-chart performance: VLMs often fail to generalize after answering the original chart correctly. We find failures are most prevalent when updated charts require novel visual reasoning pathways.

</details>


### [90] [When Does Demographic Information Help? Data and Modeling Regimes for Perspective-Aware Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27313)
*Weibin Cai,Reza Zafarani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了人口统计信息在主观标注任务（如仇恨言论检测）中何时对模型性能有帮助，发现在低训练分歧、高测试分歧、细粒度歧义测量、充足训练数据和更高的人口统计重叠度时收益最大，并提出了一种门控残差模型，将人口统计作为对纯文本预测的选择性调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口统计特征在主观任务（如仇恨言论检测）中的效用不一致——有时提升性能，有时反而引入噪声。现有研究对何时有效缺乏系统理解，本文旨在探究人口统计特征在何种数据分割属性和建模框架下能带来性能增益。

Method: 1) 分析数据分割属性对人口统计增益的影响，包括标注者分歧度、训练集大小、训练-测试人口统计覆盖度等。2) 提出门控人口统计残差模型（Gated Demographic Residual Model），将人口统计信息作为对纯文本预测的选择性调整，仅在需要时激活。

Result: 在MHS和POPQUORN数据集上的实验表明：人口统计收益集中在低训练分歧、高测试分歧、细粒度歧义测量、充足训练数据和更大人口统计重叠度的场景中。门控残差模型在高分歧或低置信度的样本上尤其有效。

Conclusion: 人口统计特征不应默认有用，其价值取决于数据场景和建模框架的共同作用。研究结果为何时以及如何有效利用标注者人口统计信息提供了指导。

Abstract: Demographic information is often used to model annotator perspectives in subjective tasks such as hate speech detection, but its benefit is inconsistent: it improves performance in some settings and behaves as noise in others. This paper asks when demographic features help. We analyze demographic gain as a function of both data split properties and modeling frameworks. For data splits, we measure annotator disagreement, namely how often annotators assign different labels to the same example, along with training size and train-test demographic coverage. We find that demographic gains concentrate in regimes with low training disagreement, high test disagreement, fine-grained ambiguity measurement, sufficient training data, and greater demographic overlap. Motivated by these regimes, we introduce a gated demographic residual model that treats demographics as a selective adjustment to text-only predictions. Experiments on MHS and POPQUORN show that this design is effective, especially on high disagreement or low confidence examples. Overall, our results suggest that demographics should not be assumed useful by default; their value depends jointly on the data regime and the modeling framework.

</details>


### [91] [Real Images, Worse Judgments: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Concreteness and Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27315)
*Yifan Jiang,Ruoxi Ning,Sheng Yao,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑视觉输入在多模态语言理解中的有效性，发现真实图像上下文不仅不能提升、反而会损害视觉-语言模型（VLM）在词汇判断任务中与人类评分的对齐，尤其在视觉证据相关性最低时影响最严重。研究通过探测分析、典型相关分析及归因案例表明，真实图像会导致表征偏移和对虚假视觉线索的过度敏感，削弱词汇属性的可恢复性。仅关注文本的推理指令可缓解这种退化。结论指出现有指令微调VLM需要更好校准视觉上下文的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验视觉输入是否真的能改进多模态模型的语言理解能力，挑战"视觉信息总是有益"的常见假设。

Method: 使用人类具体性和意象性评分作为基准，对比VLM在有/无真实图像上下文时的词汇判断表现；采用探测分析、典型相关分析和归因案例研究来分析表征变化和虚假关联。

Result: 真实图像上下文未带来一致增益，反而降低与人类评分的对齐度；图像相关性越低，损害越严重；真实图像导致表征偏移和对虚假视觉线索的敏感度上升，削弱词汇属性可恢复性；文本专注指令可部分缓解退化。

Conclusion: 当前指令微调VLM需要更好的校准机制来判别何时视觉上下文应参与词汇判断，避免视觉信息对语言理解造成负面影响。

Abstract: Visual inputs are often assumed to improve language understanding in multimodal models. We examine this assumption by asking whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish useful visual evidence from incidental image context in lexical judgments. We use human concreteness and imagery ratings because they span words with varying expected visual relevance, from abstract and low-imagery words to concrete and high-imagery words. We find that real-image contexts do not yield consistent gains and often hurt alignment with human ratings, most sharply when visual evidence is least relevant. Through probing and canonical correlation analysis, complemented by an attribution case study, we find that real-image contexts are associated with representational shifts and greater sensitivity to spurious visual cues, coinciding with weaker recoverability of the targeted lexical properties. We further show that instructing models to focus solely on textual content at inference time can reduce this degradation, with the clearest gains on these vulnerable subsets. Our findings suggest that current instruction-tuned VLMs need better calibration of when visual context should inform lexical judgments.

</details>


### [92] [Semantic Gradients Interactions in SSD: A Case Study in Racial Identity and Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27322)
*Felix Ostrowicki,Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Interaction SSD扩展了监督语义差异法，用于建模语义意义在不同调节变量（如群体、特质或条件）下的变化，使这种变化可检验且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSD方法无法检验语义意义是否以及如何随调节变量（如标注者种族身份）变化，导致对群体间语义差异的统计检验和可解释性不足。

Method: 提出Interaction SSD，估计主语义梯度、交互梯度和条件梯度，所有梯度均可通过标准SSD工具解释。在UC Berkeley仇恨言论语料库上验证了标注者种族身份对仇恨言论判断的调节作用。

Result: 交互模型检测到显著的调节效应：共享梯度区分了非人化敌意与反言论，而交互梯度揭示了群体间在哪些语义线索预测仇恨言论评分上存在较小的差异。

Conclusion: Interaction SSD使得调节变量对意义-结果关系的统计检验和解释成为可能，为分析群体间语义理解差异提供了可操作的工具。

Abstract: We introduce interaction SSD, an extension of Supervised Semantic Differential that models how semantic meaning varies across moderators such as groups, traits, or conditions making this variation testable and interpretable. The method estimates a main semantic gradient, an interaction gradient, and conditional gradients, all interpretable through standard SSD tools. We illustrate it on the UC Berkeley Measuring Hate Speech corpus, testing whether annotator racial identity moderates hate-speech judgments of comments targeting people of color. The interaction model detects a significant moderation effect: the shared gradient contrasts dehumanizing hostility with counter-speech, while the interaction gradient reveals smaller group-linked differences in which semantic cues predict hate-speech ratings. Interaction SSD makes moderated meaning-outcome relationships statistically testable and interpretable.

</details>


### [93] [FinHarness: An Inline Lifecycle Safety Harness for Finance LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27333)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Yingguang Yang,Yancheng Chen,Jiayu Liang,Qian Li,Hanning Lu,Kefu Xu,Hao Zheng,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Peng,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinHarness 是一个面向金融大模型智能体的内联安全防护框架，通过查询监控、工具监控和级联评分模块，将风险因子重新注入智能体输入，实现事前干预。在 FinVault 基准上，ASR 从 38.3% 降至 15.0%，良性批准率从 41.1% 仅降至 39.3%，且高级裁判调用次数减少 4.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域的 LLM 智能体必须同时阻止提示注入导致的未授权操作，同时批准合法的多步业务流程。现有边界过滤器常遗漏不可逆的中间轨迹工具调用，而事后 LLM 裁判仅在终止后审计，无法干预且计算成本随轨迹长度线性增长。

Method: 提出 FinHarness，包含三个组件：1) Query Monitor：融合单轮意图与跨轮漂移检测；2) Tool Monitor：评估每个待执行工具调用；3) Cascade 模块：集成每步风险并自适应地在轻量级与高级 LLM 裁判之间路由验证。触发风险因子以事前证据形式重新注入智能体输入，使其自行拒绝、重新规划或批准。

Result: 在 FinVault 基准上，FinHarness 将攻击成功率 (ASR) 从 38.3% 降至 15.0%，良性批准率从 41.1% 轻微降至 39.3%，且相比始终使用高级裁判的方案，高级裁判调用次数减少 4.7 倍。

Conclusion: FinHarness 通过内联安全防护和自适应路由验证，在保障金融智能体安全性的同时有效控制了计算开销。

Abstract: Finance LLM agents must simultaneously block prompt-induced unauthorized actions and approve legitimate multi-step business workflows. However, boundary filters often miss irreversible mid-trajectory tool calls, while post-hoc LLM judges perform auditing only after termination -- too late for intervention and at a computational cost that scales linearly with trace length. We present FinHarness, an inline safety harness that wraps a finance agent end-to-end with three components: a Query Monitor that fuses single-turn intent with cross-turn drift, a Tool Monitor that evaluates each prospective tool call, and a Cascade module that integrates per-step risk and adaptively routes verification between a lightweight and an advanced-tier LLM judge. Fired risk factors are re-injected into the agent input as ex-ante evidence, enabling the agent to refuse, re-plan, or approve on its own. On FinVault, routed FinHarness cuts ASR from 38.3% to 15.0% while largely preserving benign approval ($41.1\% \to 39.3\%$), and uses $4.7\times$ fewer advanced-judge calls than an always-advanced ablation.

</details>


### [94] [MATCHA: Matching Text via Contrastive Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27345)
*Siran Li,Ece Sena Etoglu,Carsten Eickhoff,Seyed Ali Bahrainian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有评估指标（ROUGE、BERTScore等）无法区分语义矛盾，提出MATCHA——一种联合奖励语义一致性与惩罚矛盾的自动评估指标，在八个公开基准上优于现有方法，在TruthfulQA上相比ROUGE-L和BERTScore分别提升18.38%和20.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore）无法准确判断文档的语义相似性，经常给相互矛盾的文本分配几乎相同的分数，从而掩盖了模型生成内容中的根本性错误。

Method: 提出MATCHA指标，采用双视角方法：一方面衡量与参考文本（金标准）的语义接近度，另一方面衡量与对抗生成的矛盾文本（反事实）的语义距离，从而联合奖励语义一致性并惩罚矛盾。

Result: 在八个公共基准（涵盖问答、图像字幕生成、自然语言推理、摘要和语义文本相似性任务）上，MATCHA优于流行指标。在TruthfulQA上，相比ROUGE-L提升18.38%，相比BERTScore提升20.82%。与23种嵌入模型（包括最先进的模型）相比，MATCHA在仅依据参考文本区分正确与错误陈述方面最准确。

Conclusion: 定量比较和人工评估均证实了MATCHA的有效性和有效性，并揭示了现有评估指标的固有缺陷。MATCHA在判别正确与错误陈述方面优于现有指标和嵌入模型。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation is essential for understanding large language model (LLM) performance, yet today's go-to metrics, namely token-overlap scores (e.g., ROUGE) and embedding-based measures (e.g., BERTScore), often misjudge semantic similarity of documents. Our study shows that both token-overlap metrics and embedding-based metrics routinely assign nearly identical scores to texts that directly contradict each other, thereby potentially masking fundamental errors. We introduce MATCHA, an automatic metric that jointly rewards semantic agreement with a reference and penalizes contradictions. MATCHA employs a dual-view perspective that measures (i) proximity to the gold text and (ii) distance from an adversarially generated counterfactual contradiction. In eight public benchmarks, MATCHA outperforms popular metrics, compared with human annotations on question-answering, image caption generation, natural language inference, summarization, and semantic textual similarity tasks. On the TruthfulQA dataset (i.e., a dataset without a training set, where no embedding-based metrics could locally train on), this improvement in terms of matching texts with a reference reaches 18.38% over ROUGE-L and 20.82% over BERTScore. Both quantitative comparison and qualitative human assessments confirm the efficacy and validity of MATCHA and uncover fundamental weaknesses in pre-existing metrics. Compared with 23 embedding models, including top state-of-the-art ones, used as a metric similar to BERTScore, MATCHA remains the most accurate in distinguishing correct from incorrect statements solely based on a reference. Our code and metric are publicly available (https://github.com/Siran-Li/MATCHA).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [GEM: Geometric Entropy Mixing for Optimal LLM Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26121)
*Yue Min,Ziyun Qiao,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GEM框架，将数据配比问题重新表述为超球面上的变分问题，通过MM算法优化和师生蒸馏实现大规模数据混合，在1.1B参数模型上提升下游准确率最高达1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据混合策略受限于分类缺陷：人工分类存在本体论错位，欧氏聚类无法处理嵌入各向异性，导致难以发现最优数据配比。

Method: 将数据配比建模为超球面上的变分问题，引入混合平衡正则化项；通过MM算法解耦生成先验并优化目标函数；利用师生蒸馏实现几何保真度的规模化扩展；提出几何影响分数(GIS)用于可解释的类别生成。

Result: 在1.1B参数模型上，GEM集成到DoReMi和RegMix等混合策略中，平均下游准确率提升高达1.2%，建立了新的SOTA。

Conclusion: GEM为可预测的数据混合提供了鲁棒的坐标系统，有效解决了嵌入各向异性和聚类坍缩问题，证明了几何感知的数据配比方法优于传统欧氏启发式方法。

Abstract: LLM pre-training efficacy increasingly depends on data composition rather than sheer volume. Yet, optimal mixing is hindered by categorization flaws: human taxonomies suffer from ontological misalignment, and Euclidean clustering fails to address embedding anisotropy. We introduce GEM (Geometric Entropy Mixing), a framework reformulating data curation as a variational problem on the hypersphere augmented with a mixing-balance regularizer. By decoupling the generative prior and optimizing the objective via a provable MM (Minorize-Maximize) algorithm, GEM effectively counteracts the cluster collapse to discover balanced semantic structures invisible to Euclidean heuristics. We employ teacher-student distillation to scale this geometric fidelity to web-scale corpora and introduce the Geometric Influence Score (GIS) for interpretable taxonomy generation. Experiments with 1.1B-parameter models demonstrate that GEM establishes a new state-of-the-art when integrated into mixing strategies like DoReMi and RegMix, improving average downstream accuracy by up to 1.2% and offering a robust coordinate system for predictable data mixing.

</details>


### [96] [AirCast-SR: A Foundation Model for Kilometer-Scale Atmospheric Super-Resolution via Latent Consistency Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26130)
*Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Joshua Durkee,Abdullah Al Fahad,Naveen Sudharsan,Prabhjot Singh,Cenlin He,Harsh Kamath,Zong-Liang Yang,Krishnagopal Halder,Sandeep Juneja,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Saptarishi Dhanuka,Amit Kumar Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EarthMind-SR（文中亦称AirCast-SR），一种基于潜一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架与3D U-Net的大气超分辨率基础模型，可将0.25°（~28 km）的全球AI天气预报降尺度至1 km、每小时分辨率，生成67小时含8个地表耦合变量的预报，在美国大陆训练并在印度和德国实现零样本全球迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公里级业务天气预测对传统数值天气预报（NWP）计算成本过高，限制了能源、农业、灾害管理等需要精细时空细节领域的预报可及性。

Method: 使用三维U-Net结合潜一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架，以GraphCast预报为输入、NOAA的AORC为训练目标，基于美国大陆（CONUS）的块状样本训练，实现从0.25°到1 km水平的降尺度。

Result: 所有变量和预报提前时间上接近零偏差；径向功率谱密度分析表明在10 km至100 km波长范围内保持精细大气结构；在美国三个季节案例上验证，并在印度和德国实现零样本全球迁移。

Conclusion: 作为开源权重的基础模型，EarthMind-SR建立了公里级AI天气预测的新范式，为区域微调、蒸馏及气候服务和灾害预报等下游应用提供平台。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction at kilometer scales remains computationally prohibitive for traditional numerical weather prediction (NWP) models, limiting forecast access for applications in energy, agriculture, and disaster management that require fine-grained spatiotemporal detail. Here we introduce AirCast-SR, a foundation model for atmospheric super-resolution that downscales global AI weather forecasts from 0.25 degree (~28 km) to 1 km horizontal resolution at hourly temporal resolution, producing 67-hour forecasts of eight coupled surface variables simultaneously. EarthMind-SR employs a three-dimensional U-Net conditioned within a Latent Consistency Model (LCM) diffusion framework, trained on patch-based samples over the contiguous United States (CONUS) using GraphCast forecasts as input and NOAA's Analysis of Record for Calibration (AORC) as the target. The model achieves near-zero bias across all variables and lead times, and its radial power spectral density analysis demonstrates preservation of fine-scale atmospheric structure at wavelengths of 10 km to 100 km where coarser models lose spectral power. We validate EarthMind-SR across three CONUS case studies spanning winter, summer, and spring seasons, and demonstrate zero-shot global transferability over India and Germany using independent surface station observations without any retraining or fine-tuning. As an open-weights foundation model, EarthMind-SR establishes a new paradigm for kilometer-scale AI weather prediction and provides a platform for regional fine-tuning, distillation, and downstream applications in climate services and hazard forecasting.

</details>


### [97] [SilIF: Silhouette-Augmented Isolation Forest for Unsupervised Transaction Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26135)
*Venkatakrishnan Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SilIF，一种基于轮廓系数的隔离森林增强方法。该方法通过树路径长度向量聚类生成轮廓分数，与原始 IF 分数加权结合。在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测数据集上（~59万笔交易，3.5%欺诈率），SilIF 在 AUC-PR 上平均提升 0.0080，但在合成数据集上无提升。论文诚实报告了生效与失效的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测在标签稀缺的交易欺诈检测中广泛使用，Isolation Forest 因其可扩展性和易部署性成为最流行的经典方法之一，但仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出 SilIF，在 Isolation Forest 基础上添加基于轮廓系数的评分层。具体方法：(1) 为每个样本提取树路径长度向量（每棵树一个路径长度）；(2) 对这些"指纹"向量进行聚类，形成结构分组；(3) 计算轮廓系数，衡量样本与所属分组及最近邻分组的匹配程度；(4) 通过单一超参数 alpha 将轮廓信号与基础 IF 分数加权结合。

Result: 在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测基准（~59万笔交易，3.5%欺诈率）上，SilIF (alpha=1.0) 在五个种子上的 AUC-PR 平均提升 0.0080，所有种子均优于原始 IF（配对t检验p=0.046）。在合成信用卡数据集 Sparkov 上，轮廓增强未带来提升。

Conclusion: SilIF 是一种可调参、易部署的 Isolation Forest 增强方法，在特定条件下有效。论文诚实报告了生效与失效的情况，并刻画了区分两类结果的条。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used in transaction fraud detection where labels are scarce. Isolation Forest (IF) is among the most popular classical methods due to its scalability and ease of deployment. We propose SilIF, an augmentation of Isolation Forest that adds a silhouette-based scoring layer computed in a representation space induced by the trees of the forest. For each point, we extract a vector of per-tree path lengths, cluster these "fingerprints" into structural groups, and compute a silhouette score that measures how well the point fits its assigned group versus the nearest alternative. The silhouette signal is combined with the base IF score via a single hyperparameter alpha. On the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark (~590K transactions, 3.5% fraud), SilIF with alpha=1.0 improves over plain Isolation Forest by +0.0080 AUC-PR on average across five seeds, with SilIF winning on all five seeds (paired t-test p=0.046). We also report results on a synthetic credit-card dataset (Sparkov) where the silhouette augmentation does not improve over plain IF, and we characterize the conditions that distinguish the two outcomes. The paper presents SilIF as a tunable, easy-to-deploy enhancement to Isolation Forest with honest reporting of when it helps and when it does not. Code at https://github.com/venkat15vk/silif-anomaly-detection.

</details>


### [98] [On the Push-Based Asynchronous Federated Learning: A Bias-Correction Aggregation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26162)
*Jiahui Bai,Hai Dong,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PushCen-ADFL是一个通信高效的异步去中心化联邦学习框架，通过质心表示空间中的耦合通信、聚合与局部稳定，在非对称通信和延迟参与下实现稳定训练，在异构数据场景下准确率提升高达6%，单次推送通信成本降低80%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步去中心化联邦学习（ADFL）存在三个核心问题：频繁的点对点通信开销大、有向拓扑上的异步更新导致聚合偏差、以及非独立同分布（non-IID）数据引发严重的模型漂移。现有方法未能同时解决通信效率、聚合偏差和模型稳定性之间的权衡。

Method: PushCen-ADFL通过以下关键技术解决上述问题：1）在共享的质心表示空间中耦合通信、聚合和局部稳定化，形成压缩与优化的闭环；2）客户端交换质心形式的消息，采用平均保持的push-sum混合机制校正聚合偏差；3）引入轻量级质心正则化（锚定在同一质心空间）以缓解异构性和陈旧性导致的漂移；4）设计有界、发送端去重的缓冲区，增强对不规则异步到达的鲁棒性。

Result: 在视觉数据集上的实验表明，PushCen-ADFL在数据异构场景下准确率提升高达6%，同时单次推送通信成本降低超过80%，实现了良好的准确率-通信权衡。

Conclusion: PushCen-ADFL通过质心空间中的通信-聚合-稳定化闭环设计，有效解决了ADFL在非对称通信和异构数据下的通信效率与模型漂移问题，在准确率和通信开销之间取得了优越的平衡。

Abstract: Asynchronous decentralized federated learning (ADFL) eliminates central coordination and global synchronization, making it attractive for large-scale and heterogeneous systems. However, frequent peer-to-peer communication, asynchronous updates on directed topologies, and non-IID data jointly lead to excessive communication overhead, biased aggregation and severe model drift. We propose PushCen-ADFL, a communication-efficient ADFL framework that enables stable training under asymmetric communication and delayed client participation. PushCen-ADFL couples communication, aggregation, and local stabilization in a shared centroid representation space, forming a closed loop between compression and optimization. Clients exchange centroid-form messages, apply average-preserving push-sum mixing to correct aggregation bias, and use a lightweight centroid regularization anchored in the same centroid space to mitigate drift under heterogeneity and staleness. A bounded, sender-deduplicated buffer further improves robustness under irregular asynchronous arrivals. Experiments on vision datasets demonstrate that PushCen-ADFL improves accuracy under data heterogeneity by up to 6\% while reducing per-push communication cost by more than 80\%, achieving a favorable accuracy-communication trade-off.

</details>


### [99] [When Rule Violations Are Rare: Chimera Training for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26171)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于逻辑规则的神经评估器用于语义异常检测，通过将约束编译为有向无环图、学习特征感知的子图MLP门控，并引入嵌合体训练策略（在特征层面进行操作数级反事实构造），解决了训练中真实异常样本稀缺和捷径学习问题，在CLEVRER、OpenImages和VidOR上提升了组合与关系规则的异常检测AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际异常不仅是罕见输入，更是语义约束的违反（如对象共现、动作前提、事件时序关系等），而传统方法难以处理这些结构化的规则违反，且训练时真实规则违反样本稀缺。

Method: 1) 将逻辑约束编译为有向无环图；2) 为内部逻辑算子学习特征感知的子图MLP门控，映射子特征与边缘级否定到父表示和规则满足概率；3) 通过真实概念标签的精确布尔传播提供中间监督；4) 提出嵌合体训练：在特征层面拼接不同样本的子图特征，每个操作数保留其来源样本的硬标签，通过逻辑算子计算嵌合体目标，提供监督信号而不需要真实异常图像。

Result: 在CLEVRER、OpenImages和VidOR上，该方法在规则级异常检测AUROC上优于独立事件和同图语义训练基线，尤其对组合规则和关系规则提升显著。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效检测语义约束违反，生成标量异常分数和规则级归因，解决了训练数据覆盖不足和捷径学习问题。

Abstract: Many practical anomalies are not merely rare inputs, but violations of semantic constraints: objects co-occur in structured ways, actions imply preconditions, and events satisfy temporal or relational regularities. We study anomaly detection in this setting, where constraints are given as logical rules over learned visual concepts, but real rule violations are rare or absent during training. We propose a neural rule evaluator that compiles each constraint into a directed acyclic graph and learns feature-aware subtree MLP gates for its internal logical operators. Each gate maps child features and edge-level negations to a parent representation and a rule-satisfaction probability, with intermediate supervision obtained from exact Boolean propagation over ground-truth concept labels. The key difficulty is that same-image training data often provide insufficient coverage of informative truth configurations and also allow shortcut solutions. To address this, we introduce chimera training: an operand-level counterfactual construction at the feature level. Instead of mixing input images, we concatenate subtree features from different samples; each operand keeps the hard truth label of the sample it came from, and the chimera target is obtained by applying the node's logical operator to those inherited labels. This supplies supervised logical counterexamples without requiring real anomalous images. Across CLEVRER, OpenImages, and VidOR, the resulting evaluator improves rule-level anomaly AUROC over independent-events and same-image semantic-training baselines, especially for compositional and relational rules. The method yields both scalar anomaly scores and rule-level attributions.

</details>


### [100] [InfoQuant: Shaping Activation Distributions for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26175)
*Ke Li,Dong An,Xiaoling Zang,Can Ye,Liang Xie,Qibo Qiu,Chen Shen,Xiaofei He,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出InfoQuant，一种无需训练的低比特激活量化方法，通过峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）将激活分布塑造成更利于量化的形式，在W4A4KV4设置下平均保留97%的浮点精度，并将LLaMA-2 13B性能差距较先前最优方法缩小42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特激活量化是高效部署大语言模型的主要瓶颈，现有后训练量化方法虽然抑制异常值、平衡通道或最小化重构误差，但很少明确什么样的激活分布才易于量化，导致激活值看似平滑但量化误差仍然很大。

Method: 从信息论角度分析量化误差，提出量化友好的激活应同时具备较小的数值范围和范围内充分的分散性；据此设计无需训练的InfoQuant方法，采用峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）塑形激活分布，并引入自适应异常值令牌选择提升优化鲁棒性。

Result: 在多种LLM系列上，InfoQuant一致优于先前PTQ和端到端训练基线；在W4A4KV4设置下平均保留97%的浮点精度，将LLaMA-2 13B的性能差距较之前最优方法缩小42%。

Conclusion: 将激活变换重新定义为面向量化器的分布设计问题，从信息论视角分析量化误差，并提出有效的无需训练方法显著提升低比特激活量化性能。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization remains a major bottleneck in efficient large language model (LLM) deployment. The difficulty is not only that activations contain outliers, but that their distributions are often poorly matched to a low-bit uniform quantizer. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suppress peaks, balance channels, or minimize reconstruction error, yet they rarely specify what activation distribution is actually easy to discretize. As a result, activations may appear numerically smoother while still incurring large quantization error because the quantization range remains wide or most values collapse into a few levels near the mean. We recast activation transformation as quantizer-facing distribution design and analyze quantization error from an information-theoretic perspective. Our analysis shows that quantization-friendly activations should jointly have a smaller numerical range and sufficient dispersion within that range. Guided by this analysis, we propose InfoQuant, a train-free method that employs Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) to shape activations into more quantization-friendly distributions. We further introduce adaptive outlier-token selection to improve the robustness of PSOT during optimization. Across multiple LLM families, InfoQuant consistently outperforms prior PTQ and end-to-end training baselines. Under W4A4KV4, it preserves 97% of floating-point accuracy on average and reduces the LLaMA-2 13B performance gap by 42% over the previous state of the art. Code is available at [https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant](https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant)

</details>


### [101] [GAC: Noise-Aware Adaptive Mixing for Hybrid SFT-RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26184)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAC，一种基于噪声感知的自适应混合权重控制器，通过在线估计梯度方差和训练信号间的分歧来动态调整监督微调和强化学习的混合比例，在数学、代码、科学和逻辑基准测试中持续优于固定和规则基线，训练开销不到1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合后训练通常结合监督微调和强化学习，但固定混合调度无法适应两种信号相对噪声随时间变化的动态环境。

Method: 提出GAC（噪声感知控制器），通过在线估计梯度方差和两种训练信号之间的分歧来推导自适应混合权重，并加入平滑、先验引导和有界更新机制，同时复用现有训练张量。

Result: 在数学、代码、科学和逻辑基准测试中，GAC一致优于强固定基线和基于规则的基线，在更大模型规模上增益更显著，训练开销低于1%。

Conclusion: GAC作为一种轻量级、噪声感知的自适应混合方法，能够有效提升混合后训练的性能，且几乎不增加额外计算成本。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training usually combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, but fixed mixing schedules cannot adapt when the relative noise of the two signals changes over time. We propose GAC, a noise-aware controller that derives an adaptive mixing weight from online estimates of gradient variance and disagreement between the two training signals. The method adds smoothing, prior guidance, and bounded updates while reusing existing training tensors. Experiments on math, code, science, and logic benchmarks show that GAC consistently improves hybrid post-training over strong fixed and rule-based baselines, with larger gains at larger model scales and less than 1% training overhead.

</details>


### [102] [Max-Window Scale Estimation for Near-Lossless HiF8 W8A8 Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26189)
*Yingying Cheng,Jinquan Shi,Li Zhou,Zhiyang He,Zhaoyi Sun,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了低比特浮点量化感知训练（HiF8 W8A8 QAT）在OpenPangu-Embedded-1B模型上的两种正交失效模式：amax饱和（延迟尺度估计导致前向裁剪破坏知识敏感表征）和灾难性遗忘（激进学习率覆盖预训练常识知识），并提出相应的修复策略（64步历史窗口保守max算法DTS、500步BF16预热后以lr=10^{-5}进行QAT），最终在MMLU、HellaSwag和ARC-Challenge上仅分别下降0.43%、0.58%和0.22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化感知训练（QAT）在低比特浮点格式下存在标准训练指标无法检测的细微失效模式，亟需系统性的识别、解耦与修复。

Method: 通过延迟张量缩放（Delayed Tensor Scaling, DTS）视角，设计八组控制实验解耦两种失效模式；采用保守的64步历史窗口max算法DTS缓解amax饱和，使用500步BF16预热+lr=10^{-5}的QAT缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 最终配置在MMLU上仅下降0.43%，HellaSwag下降0.58%，ARC-Challenge下降0.22%，训练损失绝对百分比误差仅0.11%。

Conclusion: 两种修复策略既必要又充分，且训练损失无法反映这些失效模式，表明QAT评估需要更细致的指标。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) with low-bit floating-point formats enables efficient LLM deployment, yet introduces subtle failure modes invisible to standard training metrics. We present a systematic study of HiF8 W8A8 QAT for OpenPangu-Embedded-1B through the lens of Delayed Tensor Scaling (DTS). Across eight controlled experiments, we identify and disentangle two orthogonal failure modes: (i)amax saturation, where delayed scale estimates silently corrupt knowledge-sensitive representations via forward-pass clipping, and (ii)catastrophic forgetting, where an aggressive learning rate overwrites pretrained commonsense knowledge independently of quantization. Neither is detectable from training loss alone. We address amax saturation with a conservative max-algorithm DTS strategy over a 64-step history window, and mitigate forgetting via a 500-step BF16 warmup followed by QAT at lr=10^{-5}. Both fixes are necessary and sufficient: our final configuration achieves 0.43% MMLU drop, 0.58% HellaSwag drop, and 0.22% ARC-Challenge drop versus a matched BF16 baseline, with a training loss APE of only 0.11% over 10,000 steps.

</details>


### [103] [Modeling Dynamic Mixtures of Time-Delay Systems from Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26191)
*Ren Fujiwara,Yasuko Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DelayMix，一种将流式时间序列视为动态时滞系统混合体的在线框架，通过固定长度的马尔可夫参数张量摘要历史状态，实现快速自适应建模，在非平稳数据上 outperforms 现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据流中存在输入输出关系清晰但系统快速变化（如环境因素或输入延迟变化导致的机制转换）的问题，传统方法在准确性、鲁棒性和内存使用之间存在权衡，难以同时兼顾。

Method: 将流式时间序列建模为动态时滞系统的混合体；使用马尔可夫参数序列构建摘要系统张量，以固定长度表示捕获系统动力学和输入输出延迟；必要时通过张量分解算法提取相关历史模型并选择最匹配当前状态的系统。

Result: 在真实数据集上，DelayMix在预测准确性和延迟适应速度方面 consistently 优于其他方法，尤其在高非平稳数据上表现突出。

Conclusion: DelayMix通过张量摘要机制在保持模型跟踪鲁棒性的同时降低了内存占用，实现了对环境变化的快速且计算高效的自适应。

Abstract: This research addresses the problem of adaptive modeling in time-series data streams with clear input-output relationships. This problem is challenging because rapid system changes (regime shifts) caused by environmental factors or input delay changes degrade model performance, and the trade-off among accuracy, robustness, and memory usage arises when using multiple small models for each time-series pattern. To address these issues, this paper presents an online framework/method that treats streaming time series as dynamic mixtures of time-delay systems. This framework maintains robustness of model tracking and reduces memory usage by summarizing past regimes using a fixed-length representation that captures both the system dynamics and input-output delays. Concretely, this approach constructs a summary system tensor using the system's Markov parameter series, capturing both dynamic behavior and delay characteristics. If necessary, a tensor decomposition algorithm extracts relevant past models from the tensor and helps select the system that best fits the current regime. This method enables rapid adaptation to environmental changes and is computationally efficient. Tests on real datasets show that DelayMix consistently outperforms other methods, achieving superior forecast accuracy and faster adaptation to delays, especially for highly non-stationary data.

</details>


### [104] [Co-folding model guided by structural proteomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26192)
*Alon Shtrikman,Nitzan Simchi,Michal Ran Shchory,Sagie Brodsky,Eran Seger,Kirill Pevzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIMS-Fold是一种推理时引导扩散框架，通过整合XL-MS和HDX-MS结构蛋白质组学数据来增强蛋白质结构生成模型，在诱导接近靶标上优于未引导的SOTA模型如Boltz-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构生成模型无法准确捕捉蛋白质复合物的正确构象状态，而这对于蛋白质设计和诱导接近药物（如抗体、PROTACs）至关重要。尽管XL-MS和HDX-MS等结构蛋白质组学技术能提供空间和动态信息，但将这些稀疏、异质的测量数据整合到现有模型中仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出AIMS-Fold框架，采用推理时引导扩散策略，在预训练扩散模型的基础上，利用XL-MS空间约束和HDX-MS溶剂可及性概览构建可微物理势能，主动引导生成采样轨迹。

Result: 单独使用XL-MS或HDX-MS约束均可提升预测精度，二者联合使用产生协同增益。在具有挑战性的诱导接近靶标上，AIMS-Fold的准确性超过纯计算、无引导的SOTA模型Boltz-2。

Conclusion: AIMS-Fold建立了一个强大的整合计算框架，可有效利用实验约束数据，适用于诱导接近药物的基于结构的药物设计。

Abstract: Protein structure generative models excel at predicting single protein static structures from sequence, but routinely fail to capture the correct conformational state of protein complexes, critical for protein design and induced proximity modalities such as antibodies and PROTACs. While structural proteomics techniques like Cross-Linking Mass Spectrometry (XL-MS) and Hydrogen-Deuterium Exchange (HDX-MS) offer valuable spatial and dynamic insights, integrating these sparse, heterogeneous measurements into these models remains an open challenge. Here, we bridge this gap by combining structural proteomics data with the rich biophysical priors learned by pretrained diffusion models. We introduce AIMS-Fold, an inference-time guided-diffusion framework that actively steers the generative sampling trajectory using differentiable physical potentials derived from XL-MS spatial restraints and HDX-MS solvent accessibility profiles. We demonstrate that these structural methods individually enhance predictive accuracy, and their integration yields synergistic improvement. Crucially, by leveraging these experimental restraints, AIMS-Fold achieves higher accuracy on challenging induced proximity targets than purely computational, unguided state-of-the-art models like Boltz-2. This establishes our framework as a powerful, integrative computational approach for the structure based drug design of induced proximity drugs. Evaluation code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [105] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAD框架，通过统一分类（Outlier Exposure）和重构（Masked Autoencoder）两种范式，利用分类模块生成概率引导的软掩码辅助重构模块，同时重构模块缓解分类模块的泛化问题，从而有效检测微妙和复杂的时间序列异常。实验表明CoAD显著优于现有方法，且轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法难以检测微妙和长时异常；OE方法受限于泛化能力差，MAE方法受限于掩码对齐问题。因此需要一种统一框架，利用两种范式的互补优势并缓解各自弱点。

Method: 提出CoAD框架，包含分类模块和重构模块的协同设计：分类模块生成概率引导的软掩码用于重构模块，重构模块反过来缓解分类模块的泛化问题。分类模块还专门设计了合适的分类粒度并引入频率信息。

Result: 在高质量基准数据集上，采用严格评估协议的实验表明CoAD显著优于现有SOTA深度学习和传统数据挖掘方法；同时模型轻量，速度明显快于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: CoAD通过统一分类与重构范式有效提升了时间序列异常检测性能，展示了深度学习在TSAD中的潜力，并具备大规模实时应用的实用价值。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [106] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PathoFM，一种编码器中心变压器，通过三种互补的预训练目标（局部完成、时间连续性、无监督上下文动态）在脊髓损伤的病理步态分析中学习跨任务类型和跨主体的泛化表示，发现动态中心的混合目标比分组/对比或仅重构目标产生更平衡的转移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列学习常受限于小规模异质队列和协议漂移，而下游任务涵盖分类与回归，因此亟需研究预训练目标应施加何种归纳偏置才能使表示跨任务类型和跨主体迁移。

Method: PathoFM采用编码器中心变压器，在多元步态窗口上联合训练三个互补目标：局部完成（重构连续掩蔽片段以强化局部结构）、时间连续性（从前缀预测掩蔽中期延续以强化平滑性和因果一致性）、无监督上下文动态（通过注意力基于主体示例窗口的支持-查询重构）。

Result: 动态中心的混合目标产生最平衡的迁移性能：分组目标有利于判别性边界但降低连续目标所需的幅度保真度，仅重构目标保留波形结构但在分类上表现不足；将局部重构与时间连续性结合，并在可获取示例时加入上下文条件，可得到鲁棒的主体泛化表示。

Conclusion: 预训练目标的选择对跨任务迁移至关重要，动态中心的目标混合策略在脊髓损伤病理步态分析中优于单一目标家族，为临床时间序列基础模型的设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention). Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [107] [The Bridge-Garden Dilemma in LLM Distillation: Why Mixing Hard and Soft Labels Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26246)
*Guanghui Wang,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Jin-Wen Wu,Longtao Huang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bridge-Garden分解理论，揭示在知识蒸馏中混合硬标签与软标签的优势源于降低暴露偏差，而非更紧密地匹配教师模型。基于该理论开发的混合监督方法在推理和编码任务上超越现有基线，同时降低9.7倍训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软标签理论上包含更丰富的信息，但实践中混合硬标签和软标签始终能取得更好结果，且这一增益无法用更紧密的教师匹配来解释。本文旨在揭示这种现象背后的根本原因，并基于此开发更有效的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出Bridge-Garden分解理论，将生成步骤分为两类：Bridge（必须精确匹配的步骤）和Garden（可以灵活选择的步骤）。硬标签KD在Bridge中避免风险偏差，软标签KD在Garden中保持多样性。基于该理论，开发自适应平衡硬/软标签的Bridge-Garden混合监督方法。

Result: 在Qwen、Llama、Gemma、DeepSeek等七组教师-学生对上，所提方法在推理和编码基准上超越基于散度的和基于策略的KD基线，同时将训练成本降低9.7倍。

Conclusion: 混合硬软标签KD的有效性来源于降低暴露偏差，而非更好地匹配教师分布。Bridge-Garden理论为理解这种现象提供了理论基础，并指导设计了更高效的知识蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student. In language modeling, the student is trained either on tokens sampled from the teacher (hard labels) or the teacher's full next-token distribution (soft labels). Despite soft labels appear strictly richer, we find that mixing hard and soft labels consistently yields better results. Crucially, we show that this gain cannot be explained by closer teacher matching during training. Instead, it comes from reduced exposure bias, the mismatch between training and inference distributions. To explain this phenomenon, we introduce the Bridge-Garden Decomposition theory, which categorizes generation steps into two types: Bridges, where the next token must be exact, and Gardens, where it can be flexible. We show that hard-only KD excels in Bridges by avoiding risky deviations, while soft-only KD preserves diversity in Gardens. A hybrid strategy handles both cases and, as a result, reduces exposure bias across the sequence. Guided by this theory, we develop a family of Bridge-Garden hybrid supervision methods that adaptively balance hard and soft labels. Across a primary suite of seven teacher-student pairs (including Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and DeepSeek) and benchmarks in reasoning and coding, our approach outperforms divergence-based and on-policy KD baselines while reducing training cost by 9.7x, enabling efficient model compression. Code is available at https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release.

</details>


### [108] [Quantized Keys Steal Attention: Bias Correction for KV-Cache Compression in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26266)
*Tuna Tuncer,Felix Becker,Thomas Pfeil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现量化KV缓存导致视频质量下降的关键原因是注意力权重的系统性偏差（Jensen偏差），并提出一种逐注意力分数的在线校正方法，在INT2量化下恢复接近BF16的视频质量，且额外计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块级自回归视频扩散模型中KV缓存随视频增长成为内存瓶颈，量化虽能减少内存压力但会降低视频质量，需要在不增加额外内存的前提下缓解量化带来的质量退化。

Method: 从理论上证明量化噪声因softmax中指数函数的凸性导致缓存键的注意力权重被膨胀（Jensen偏差），推导出基于量化步长和查询范数的逐注意力分数校正项，通过二阶泰勒近似使额外计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在MAGI-1、SkyReels-V2、HY-WorldPlay上以INT2量化进行评估，校正方法恢复了因激进量化损失的大部分质量，达到接近BF16的视频质量，且相比INT4量化节省50%内存的同时表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提校正方法有效消除了量化引起的注意力偏差，在不增加内存的前提下大幅提升低比特量化视频扩散模型的生成质量，为长视频生成中KV缓存压缩提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Chunk-wise autoregressive video diffusion models rely on a KV cache of previously generated chunks to avoid redundant computation, but this cache quickly becomes a memory bottleneck as videos grow longer. Methods that quantize the KV cache to low bitwidths reduce memory pressure but degrade video quality. We show that a key driver of this degradation is a systematic bias in attention weights: due to the convexity of the exponential in softmax attention, quantization noise inflates the contribution of cached keys, a phenomenon we call the Jensen bias. This effect causes quantized keys to steal attention mass from the unquantized current chunk. We derive a per-attention-score correction that removes this bias in expectation, computed on the fly from the quantization step sizes of the cached keys and the query norm. Using a second-order Taylor approximation, the additional computational overhead is negligible, and no additional memory is needed alongside the cache. Evaluated on MAGI-1, SkyReels-V2, and HY-WorldPlay at INT2 quantization, our correction recovers most of the quality lost to aggressive quantization, reaching near-BF16 video quality, and can outperform INT4 quantization while using 50% less memory.

</details>


### [109] [Scaling World-Model Reinforcement Learning Through Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26282)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Wenxuan Yuan,Zhancun Mu,Yuanzhao Zhang,Yiming Yang,Hai Wang,Zhuo Sun,Che Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MBDPO提出了一种基于扩散策略的世界模型强化学习方法，通过统一搜索与策略优化来解决结构不对齐问题，在多任务离线预训练、在线学习和离线到在线微调中均表现优异，且模型容量越大性能越好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的强化学习方法不仅面临模型偏差和误差累积问题，更关键的是存在搜索与价值学习之间的结构不对齐——策略改进依赖于由非搜索策略诱导的价值函数，导致训练不一致和次优学习。

Method: 提出MBDPO框架，将策略优化重构为潜在世界模型中搜索轨迹上的扩散过程，通过从收集的数据集中提取隐式能量函数来锚定策略，并利用扩散过程细化评分场以实现策略优化，从而缓解结构不对齐问题。

Result: 在多任务离线预训练、在线学习和离线到在线微调等多种设置下进行了评估；在离线场景中，通过在大规模数据集上预训练，观察到模型容量增加时性能持续单调提升。

Conclusion: MBDPO通过扩散策略表示统一了搜索与策略优化，有效解锁了世界模型在大规模策略学习中的潜力，解决了搜索与价值学习之间的结构不对齐瓶颈。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) can be effectively supported at scale through the use of world models. However, in practice, scaling such approaches remains fundamentally limited. A commonly recognized challenge is model bias and error compounding, which degrade long-horizon predictions. Beyond these issues, we identify a more critical yet underexplored bottleneck: a structural misalignment between search and value learning in existing world model approaches. In particular, policy improvement often relies on value functions induced by a separate, non-search policy, resulting in training inconsistency and ultimately suboptimal learning. To address this limitation, we propose Model-Based Diffusion Policy Optimization (MBDPO) in world models, a framework that unifies search and policy optimization through diffusion policy representations, thereby unlocking the potential of world models for scalable policy learning. Instead of constructing an explicit planner over a learned world model, we reformulate policy optimization as a diffusion process over searched trajectories in latent world models. In this view, we extract an implicit energy function from the collected dataset that anchors the policy, enabling MBDPO to refine the score field for policy optimization while mitigating misalignment. We evaluate MBDPO across a wide range of settings, including multi-task offline pretraining, online learning, and offline-to-online fine-tuning. In the offline regime, we further investigate its scaling behavior by pretraining on large-scale datasets, observing consistent and monotonic performance gains with increasing model capacity.

</details>


### [110] [Two-Parameter Flows for Learning Population Dynamics of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26285)
*Paul Schwerdtner,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需轨迹数据、仅利用无标签样本学习高维概率密度随时间演化动力学的方法。通过引入双参数流，先从基分布学习到各边际分布的采样时间传输，再通过回归耦合的合成轨迹提取物理时间速度场，证明了该动力学唯一性且继承正则性。方法可扩展至高维，避免每步最优传输耦合，并支持非梯度动力学（如旋转/循环物理现象）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在缺乏轨迹信息（仅有非时序无标签样本）时难以学习高维概率密度的动力学，尤其是无法处理旋转/循环等非梯度物理现象。作者希望构建一种无需轨迹数据、可扩展至高频、支持非梯度动力学的密度演化学习方法。

Method: 提出双参数流框架：（1）采样时间流：利用条件流匹配技术从基分布学习到每个观测边际分布的传输映射；（2）物理时间流：通过生成合成轨迹对（将采样时间映射逆推为物理时间轨迹），回归拟合物理时间速度场。理论证明该物理时间动力学唯一且继承采样时间传输的正则性。

Result: 理论上证明了所提物理时间动力学的唯一性及其对采样时间传输正则性的继承性。方法可扩展到高维空间，无需每一步计算最优传输耦合，且能学习非梯度动力学（如旋转/循环现象）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种从无标签、无轨迹样本中学习高维概率密度动力学的有效范式，兼具理论保证与实用扩展性，尤其适用于存在旋转/循环等非梯度物理过程的应用场景。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning the dynamics of high-dimensional probability densities over time using unlabeled samples, without assuming access to trajectory information. We introduce two-parameter flows that learn only sampling-time transports from a base distribution to each marginal and then extract a physics-time velocity by regressing on coupled synthetic trajectories. We prove that the resulting physics-time dynamics are unique and inherit regularity from the sampling-time transports. Because we can build on standard, well-developed conditional flow matching techniques for learning the base-to-marginal transports, our approach scales to high dimensions and avoids per-step optimal-transport couplings, while allowing admissible non-gradient dynamics that can naturally explain rotational or circulating physics phenomena.

</details>


### [111] [Stateful Inference for Low-Latency Multi-Agent Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26289)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种有状态推理架构，将传统服务中每轮O(n_t)的对话成本降低为仅处理增量token的O(Δ_t)成本，通过持久化KV缓存、基数前缀缓存和提示查找推测解码，在6轮和35轮多智能体工作流中分别实现了每轮2.1倍和4.2倍的加速，端到端时间减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理框架将每次工具调用视为独立请求，即使85-95%的提示内容与上一轮相同，仍需从头重新处理整个对话，导致大量冗余计算。

Method: 提出有状态推理架构：(1) 持久化KV缓存跨轮次存活，仅摄取新token；(2) 基数前缀缓存扩展至交错的多智能体流量；(3) 提示查找推测解码加速结构化输出。

Result: 在6轮工作流中每轮加速2.1倍，35轮工作流中中位数轮次加速4.2倍，端到端时间减半。

Conclusion: 性能提升来源于有状态复用和推测解码，而非传统缓存机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent tool calling is becoming the dominant interaction pattern for LLM-based systems, yet existing inference frameworks treat each tool call as an independent request, re-processing the entire conversation from scratch even though 85-95% of the prompt is unchanged from the previous turn.
  We present a stateful inference architecture that converts the $O(n_t)$ per-turn cost of conventional serving into an $O(Δ_t)$ delta-only cost: a persistent KV cache lives across turns and advances by ingesting only the new tokens, while a radix prefix cache extends this across interleaved multi-agent traffic and a prompt-lookup speculative decoder accelerates structured output. Against vLLM and SGLang on novel, fully-generated workloads, the reference implementation is $2.1\times$ faster per turn on a 6-turn agentic workflow and $4.2\times$ on the median turn of a 35-turn one, halving end-to-end wall time. The advantage comes from stateful reuse and speculation, not caching.

</details>


### [112] [Curriculum Learning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26315)
*Sandeep Kumar,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Staged-Competence框架，通过课程学习（按难度组织偏好数据、基于能力采样、渐进更新参考模型）提升DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性，在三个模型家族上平均降低16%的OOD有害响应率和20%的越狱攻击成功率，仅用75%数据即可匹配基线安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO在安全对齐中表现脆弱，存在较差的分布外泛化问题，亟需提升其鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Staged-Competence课程学习框架：(1) 按难度组织偏好数据；(2) 采用基于能力的采样策略；(3) 训练过程中渐进式更新参考模型。

Result: 在三个模型家族上平均降低16%的OOD有害响应率和20%的越狱攻击成功率，同时保持通用能力且近乎零过度拒绝；仅用75%训练数据即可匹配基线安全性能；在安全/不安全响应间实现更好分离。

Conclusion: Staged-Competence框架有效提升了DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性和泛化能力，且与策略优化损失无关，可扩展到其他DPO变体和对齐领域。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) is widely used for safety alignment in large language models. However, prior work shows it is brittle and exhibits poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation. In this paper, we investigate whether Curriculum Learning can improve the robustness of DPO-based safety alignment. We propose Staged-Competence, a curriculum-based framework that organises preference data by difficulty, employs competence-based sampling, and progressively updates the reference model during training. Averaged across three model families, Staged-Competence reduces OOD harmful response rates by 16% and jailbreak attack success rates by 20%, while preserving general capabilities with near-zero over-refusal. We further show that Staged-Competence (1) matches baseline safety with only 75% of the training data and (2) yields better separation between safe and unsafe responses. Staged-Competence is agnostic to the policy optimisation loss and can extend to other DPO variants and alignment domains. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Sandeep5500/curriculum-learning-for-safety.

</details>


### [113] [Semigroup Consistency as a Diagnostic for Learned Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26324)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化半群误差（Normalized Semigroup Error）作为评估学习型物理模拟器长程 rollout 性能的诊断指标，通过比较直接演化与组合演化的预测一致性来捕捉时序组合失败，并在热方程和Burgers动力学上验证了其与 rollout 退化的正向关联（Spearman ρ=0.635）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要依赖一步或短时预测误差，无法捕捉时序组合和长程 rollout 中的失败模式，需要一种能诊断长期预测退化的评估方法。

Method: 利用自治状态完备系统的半群性质，提出归一化半群误差，比较直接 s+t 步演化与先 s 步再 t 步组合演化的预测差异，作为与模型无关的后验诊断指标。

Result: 在一维热方程和Burgers方程上，使用ConvNet和FNO基线，半群误差与 rollout 退化呈正相关，轨迹级Spearman相关系数 ρ=0.635（95% CI [0.621, 0.649]）。半群正则化效果不一，更适合作为评估诊断而非通用训练目标。

Conclusion: 半群误差是一种有效的长程 rollout 诊断工具，但作为训练正则化项的效果有限，主要价值在于评估而非训练。

Abstract: Learned physics simulators are often evaluated by one-step or short-horizon prediction error, but these metrics can miss failures in temporal composition and long-horizon rollout. For autonomous, state-complete systems, exact solution maps satisfy a semigroup law: direct evolution over $s+t$ should agree with evolution over $s$ followed by $t$. We propose normalized semigroup error as a post hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic comparing these direct and composed learned predictions. On one-dimensional heat and Burgers dynamics with time-conditioned ConvNet and FNO baselines, semigroup error is positively associated with rollout degradation, with trajectory-level Spearman correlation $ρ= 0.635$ and $95%$ CI $[0.621, 0.649]$. Semigroup regularization has mixed effects, supporting semigroup consistency primarily as an evaluation diagnostic rather than a universally beneficial training objective.

</details>


### [114] [Reparametrizing Shampoo and SOAP for Subspace Basis Updates and BFloat16 Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26327)
*Alan Milligan,Zikun Xu,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Felix Dangel,Wu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种重参数化策略，通过子空间部分QR分解和BFP16存储，在保持性能的同时降低Shampoo类优化器的计算和内存开销，使KL-SOAP在BFP16下能匹配或超越KL-Shampoo。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shampoo类方法（如KL-Shampoo、SOAP）依赖QR分解，但现有QR实现需要FP32精度且计算昂贵，导致大预条件矩阵时时间和内存开销大；使用BFP16虽能降低内存但会损害性能。

Method: 提出预条件矩阵的重参数化方案：支持BFP16存储，将更新后的基向量与未变化的基向量组合形成完整基；通过在子空间中对部分基进行QR分解来减少计算开销，并缓解BFP16存储带来的性能下降。

Result: 该方法广泛适用于使用QR分解的Shampoo类方法（KL-Shampoo、SOAP、KL-SOAP），尤其在BFP16下提升了SOAP和KL-SOAP的性能，使KL-SOAP达到或超过KL-Shampoo。

Conclusion: 所提方法使Shampoo类优化器在内存和时间效率上更优。

Abstract: Shampoo-based methods, such as KL-Shampoo and SOAP, have demonstrated strong performance in training neural networks and rely on QR decomposition.
