### [151] [MTL-FNO: A Lightweight Multi-Task Fourier Neural Operator for Sparse Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26718)
*Siyu Ye,Shihang Li,Zhiqiang Gong,Benrong Zhang,Weien Zhou,Yiyong Huang,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级多任务傅里叶神经算子（MTL-FNO），通过硬参数共享、低秩任务特定微调、极坐标分解解耦优化以及Cayley变换重参数化，在少样本条件下实现多场稀疏重建，模型尺寸减少60%-76%的同时保持或超越标准FNO的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在航天器机载多场稀疏重建中，部署独立模型会导致模型尺寸过大且无法利用跨场相关性，尤其在少样本条件下问题更为突出。

Method: 1) 提出MTL-FNO，基于硬参数共享的端到端联合训练框架，每层参数分为共享和任务特定两部分，任务特定参数采用低秩项实现模型压缩；2) 从极坐标角度重新审视FNO谱权重，通过极分解将谱权重解耦为编码相位的酉张量和表征幅值的半正定张量，分阶段优化以缓解任务冲突；3) 引入Cayley变换对酉张量进行重参数化，将有约束优化转化为无约束优化，保持训练中的酉几何保真度。

Result: 在两个代表性工程案例的少样本条件下，MTL-FNO实现了与标准FNO相当甚至更优的精度，同时总模型尺寸分别减少76%和60%。

Conclusion: 所提出的MTL-FNO方法有效解决了多场稀疏重建中模型尺寸过大和跨场相关性利用不足的问题，为航天器机载自主运行提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient onboard multi-field sparse reconstruction is essential for the autonomous operation of aerospace vehicles. While existing deep learning models exhibit promise for single-field reconstruction, deploying multiple independent models leads to prohibitive model size growth and fails to exploit cross-field correlations, particularly under few-shot conditions. To address these challenges, we first propose a lightweight multi-task Fourier neural operator (MTL-FNO), an end-to-end joint training framework based on hard parameter sharing. In each layer, the parameters are divided into shared and task-specific components to capture common features across fields while preserving task-specific characteristics. Moreover, the task-specific fine-tuning parameters are implemented as low-rank terms, achieving substantial model compression. Second, to address the difficulty of co-optimizing shared and task-specific parameters along with their real and imaginary parts, we revisit the FNO's spectral weight from a polar-form perspective and devise a physically meaningful decoupled optimization scheme. Specifically, we apply polar decomposition to slice-wise disentangle the spectral weight into a unitary tensor encoding phase information and a positive semi-definite tensor characterizing amplitude. By decoupling the optimization of phase and amplitude, our method can effectively mitigate tasks conflict. Meanwhile, to preserve unitary geometric fidelity during training, the Cayley transform is introduced to reparameterize the unitary tensor, converting the constrained optimization problem to an unconstrained one. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed method under few-shot conditions is validated on two representative engineering cases. Results show that MTL-FNO achieves accuracy comparable to or even surpassing that of standard FNO, while reducing total model size by 76% and 60%, respectively.

</details>


### [152] [APEX: Amplitude Anchors and Phase Priors for Target-Scarce Higher-Frequency Wave Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26732)
*Yifan Sun,Lei Cheng,Sijie Chen,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: APEX框架利用低频神经算子的振幅作为可迁移的结构锚点，结合基于格林函数相位先验的条件流匹配增强器，在目标监督稀缺条件下实现高频波场预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频波场数据的获取成本远高于低频数据，而跨频率迁移存在固有不对称性：振幅结构相对稳定，但相位敏感的振荡结构随频率升高迅速退化。现有方法在高频预测且目标监督稀缺的场景下研究不足。

Method: 提出APEX框架：首先用低频神经算子预测目标频率区域的粗场，仅保留振幅作为可迁移结构锚点；然后利用基于格林函数相位先验的条件流匹配增强器，在相位先验指导下重建目标高频场。

Result: 在SimpleWave、Helmholtz和Maxwell基准测试上，APEX在有限目标频率监督下，持续优于直接低到高外推、目标适配算子以及联合生成基线方法。

Conclusion: 高频振荡波场预测不应依赖完整复场的端到端迁移，而应显式复用可迁移的粗粒度结构，同时单独恢复缺失的振荡细节。

Abstract: Learning-based surrogates have become increasingly effective for wave-field prediction, and neural operators in particular have shown strong performance within observed frequency regimes. However, higher-frequency prediction under scarce target supervision remains comparatively underexplored, especially in wave problems where higher-frequency data are substantially more expensive to simulate or measure than lower-frequency data. A central difficulty is that cross-frequency transfer is inherently asymmetric: coarse amplitude structure remains relatively stable across frequencies, whereas phase-sensitive oscillatory structure deteriorates much more rapidly as frequency increases. Motivated by this asymmetry, we propose APEX, Amplitude-anchored and Phase-prior-guided Enhancement from eXtrapolated coarse predictions, a framework for target-scarce higher-frequency wave-field prediction. A lower-frequency neural operator first provides a coarse prediction in the target-frequency regime, from which we retain only the amplitude as a transferable structural anchor. A conditional flow-matching enhancer then reconstructs the target higher-frequency field under the guidance of a Green's-function-inspired phase prior. Experiments on SimpleWave, Helmholtz, and Maxwell benchmarks show that APEX consistently outperforms direct lower-to-higher extrapolation, target-adapted operator, and joint generative baselines under limited target-frequency supervision. Our results suggest that reliable higher-frequency prediction of oscillatory wave fields should not rely on direct end-to-end transfer of the full complex field, but instead on explicitly reusing transferable coarse structure while separately recovering the missing oscillatory detail.

</details>


### [153] [Stabilizing Recurrent Dynamics for Test-Time Scalable Latent Reasoning in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26733)
*Xiao-Wen Yang,Ziyu Han,Xi-Hua Zhang,Wen-Da Wei,Jie-Jing Shao,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STARS框架，通过雅可比谱半径正则化约束潜状态收敛到渐近稳定不动点，解决循环语言模型在深度递归时性能先升后降的不稳定问题，实现可靠的测试时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型在测试时通过深度递归进行潜在推理时，性能往往在某个迭代深度达到峰值后随递归加深而崩溃，这种不可靠的测试时扩展行为亟需解决。

Method: 通过将推理视为不确定性降低过程，提出STARS训练框架。采用高效的雅可比谱半径正则化配合随机循环采样，约束潜状态趋近于渐近稳定不动点，在保证严格稳定性的同时最大化有效性。

Result: 在算术任务上实现了可靠的测试时扩展；在复杂数学推理任务上，随着递归深度增加显著缓解了性能退化，同时提升了峰值性能。

Conclusion: 稳定性与有效性之间存在内在权衡，通过向稳定不动点收敛可以有效改善循环语言模型的测试时扩展行为，STARS框架验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) enable efficient latent reasoning through depth recurrence, yet exhibit unreliable test-time scaling behavior: performance often peaks at a certain iteration depth and then collapses with further recurrence. Through latent dynamics analysis, we find an inherent trade-off between stability and effectiveness in existing architectures and strategies. By conceptualizing reasoning as uncertainty reduction, we propose that convergence toward stable fixed points while preserving effectiveness represents a promising way. To this end, we propose STARS (STAbility-driven Recurrent Scaling), a training framework that constrains latent states to approach asymptotically stable fixed points. This is realized via efficient Jacobian Spectral Radius Regularization with random loop sampling, enabling STARS to maximize effectiveness while ensuring rigorous stability. Experiments on arithmetic tasks show that STARS achieves reliable test-time scaling, and on complex mathematical reasoning it substantially mitigates performance degradation as recurrence depth increases while also improving peak performance.

</details>


### [154] [Time Series Causal Discovery via Context-Conditioned and Causality-Augmented Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26759)
*Biao Ouyang,Tengxue Zhang,Zhihao Zhuang,Yang Shu,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PTCD，一个面向时间序列因果发现的预训练框架，通过上下文条件建模、可迁移因果增强、双尺度迭代注意力、高斯混合路由机制以及基于干预和因果混合的预训练范式，在多个真实OOD数据集上实现了优越的因果发现和根因识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果发现方法依赖数据集特定优化，难以将因果发现能力迁移到具有不同因果机制的新时间序列上，缺乏跨任务泛化能力。

Method: 1) 双尺度迭代注意力机制捕捉窗口级因果关系；2) 高斯混合模型结合上下文级路由机制处理异构外生分布；3) 基于合成数据的预训练范式，包含干预学习和因果混合策略。

Result: 在多个真实世界分布外（OOD）数据集上，PTCD在因果发现和根因识别任务中均表现出色。

Conclusion: PTCD通过预训练范式有效提升了时间序列因果发现的跨任务泛化能力，在分布偏移场景下能够稳定地进行因果发现和根因定位。

Abstract: Causal discovery from time series is critical for many real-world applications, such as tracing the root causes of anomalies. Existing approaches typically rely on dataset-specific optimization, making it difficult to transfer their causal discovery capabilities to new time series governed by diverse causal mechanisms. In this paper, we propose \textbf{PTCD}, a novel \textbf{P}retraining framework for \textbf{T}ime-series \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{D}iscovery, which improves cross-task generalization through context-conditioned modeling and transferable causal augmentation. To model complex temporal causal dependencies, PTCD employs a dual-scale iterative attention mechanism to capture window-level causal relationships, and a Gaussian mixture with a context-level routing mechanism to handle heterogeneous exogenous distributions. To further address distribution shifts across causal graphs, PTCD adopts a pretraining paradigm on synthetic datasets that integrates intervention-based learning and a causal mixup strategy, promoting stable causal discovery and stronger generalization. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets demonstrate that PTCD excels in both causal discovery and root cause identification.

</details>


### [155] [Ratio-Variance Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26784)
*Yu Luo,Shuo Han,Yihan Hu,Lei Lv,Huaping Liu,Fuchun Sun,Jianye Hao,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R²VPO，通过显式约束策略比率方差替代PPO中的启发式裁剪，在保持信任区域约束的同时保留高回报高发散更新的梯度信号，并在LLM和机器人控制任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO等on-policy算法依赖启发式裁剪来强制执行信任区域，但这种机制会不加区分地截断高回报但高发散度的更新，造成严重的性能损失。需要一种更 principled 的方法来近似信任区域约束。

Method: 通过显式约束策略比率方差（Ratio-Variance Regularization）作为信任区域约束的局部近似，替代二值的硬裁剪。采用原始-对偶优化框架（primal-dual optimization）实现该约束，起到分布式的"软刹车"作用——保留新颖探索的梯度信号，同时降低权重并允许复用陈旧/离策略数据。

Result: 在7种LLM规模（涵盖快速和慢速推理范式）和10种机器人控制任务上评估。在数学推理基准上取得显著提升，尤其在小模型上改善明显；样本效率大幅提高；在连续控制任务中持续优于PPO基线，特别是在稀疏奖励和动态环境中。

Conclusion: 比率方差正则化可作为稳定且数据高效策略优化的 principled 基础，为替代PPO启发式裁剪提供了理论上有依据且实践上有效的方法。

Abstract: Standard on-policy reinforcement learning relies on heuristic clipping to enforce trust regions, but this mechanism imposes a severe cost by indiscriminately truncating high-return yet high-divergence updates. We demonstrate that explicitly constraining the policy ratio variance provides a principled local approximation to trust-region constraints, eliminating the need for binary hard clipping. By acting as a distributional ``soft brake'', this approach preserves critical gradient signals from novel discoveries while naturally down-weighting and enabling the reuse of stale, off-policy data. We introduce ${\bf R}^2{\bf VPO}$ (Ratio-Variance Regularized Policy Optimization), which implements this constraint via a primal-dual optimization framework. Extensive evaluations across $7$ LLM scales, spanning both fast and slow reasoning paradigms, and $10$ robotic control tasks demonstrate the generality of the proposed approach. R$^2$VPO achieves substantial performance gains on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with particularly pronounced improvements on smaller models, while significantly improving sample efficiency. Furthermore, it consistently outperforms PPO baselines in continuous control domains, particularly in sparse-reward and dynamic environments. Together, these findings establish ratio-variance regularization as a principled foundation for stable and data-efficient policy optimization.

</details>


### [156] [PATE-TabTransGAN: Differentially Private Synthetic Tabular Data Generation via Transformer-Based Student Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26802)
*M. Youssef,M. Woźniak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATE-TabTransGAN是一个结合PATE机制与Transformer判别器的差分隐私表格数据生成框架，通过教师集成学习、噪声聚合标签和残差生成器实现形式化的(ε, δ)-DP保证，在四个基准数据集上取得了优于或持平现有最优方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私表格数据生成方法存在两难困境：提供强理论保护的方法牺牲了特征间依赖关系的建模能力，而擅长捕捉复杂列关系的架构仅提供经验性隐私保证。如何在保证形式化差分隐私的同时生成高保真合成表格数据仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: PATE-TabTransGAN框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 在不相交数据划分上训练的Logistic Regression教师集成，通过噪声聚合标签监督学生判别器；(2) 基于Transformer的学生判别器，用于捕获复杂的列间依赖关系；(3) 残差生成器，通过后处理继承差分隐私保证。隐私核算采用GNMax RDP accountant实现数值稳定的隐私预算计算。

Result: 在Adult、Breast、Cardio、Cervical四个基准数据集上，PATE-TabTransGAN在所有数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳的AUROC；在AUCPR指标上，在Cardio数据集上与最强基线持平，在Cervical上领先，在Breast上略逊；在Adult上观察到AUCPR差距与正类标签约定差异有关而非合成质量缺陷。

Conclusion: PATE-TabTransGAN通过将PATE机制与Transformer架构有机结合，成功同时实现了形式化差分隐私保证和高保真表格数据生成，在多个基准上达到或超越现有最优方法水平，为差分隐私合成表格数据领域提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity synthetic tabular data under formal differential privacy guarantees remains an open challenge. Methods that provide strong theoretical protection typically sacrifice the modeling of inter-feature dependencies required for realistic synthesis, while architectures that excel at capturing complex column relationships offer only empirical privacy guarantees. We present PATE-TabTransGAN, a generative framework that integrates the Private Aggregation of Teacher Ensembles (PATE) mechanism with a Transformer-based student discriminator to jointly address both requirements, and employs a GNMax RDP accountant for numerically stable privacy accounting. An ensemble of Logistic Regression teachers trained on disjoint partitions supervise the student via noisy-aggregated labels, and a residual generator is optimized against this differentially private student, inheriting formal (ε, δ)-DP guarantees by post-processing. PATE-TabTransGAN was compared with PATE-GAN, DP-GAN, and DP-CTGAN, considered state-of-the-art in differentially private tabular synthesis. Experiments conducted on four tabular benchmarks (Adult, Breast, Cardio, Cervical) confirmed the high quality of the proposed method: PATE-TabTransGAN attains the best or tied-best AUROC on all four datasets. On AUCPR it matches the strongest baseline on Cardio, leads on Cervical, and trails on Breast; on Adult, we demonstrate that AUCPR is highly sensitive to positive-class convention, and that the observed gap is consistent with a convention difference between evaluation pipelines rather than a synthesis deficit.

</details>


### [157] [Innovation: An Almost Characterization of Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26808)
*Nishant P. Das,Piyush Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入“创新”（innovation）概念刻画大语言模型产生训练数据之外输出的倾向，证明创新几乎是幻觉的充要条件，并基于缺失质量（missing mass）给出了幻觉率的新下界，扩展了Kalai和Vempala（STOC 2024）的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究校准的大语言模型为何不可避免地产生幻觉，以及放弃校准能否避免幻觉。具体回答两个根本问题：(i) 校准LLM中什么性质导致幻觉不可避免？(ii) 放弃校准能否避免幻觉？

Method: 引入“创新率”（innovation rate）这一简化概念，度量模型产生训练数据之外输出的倾向；证明创新与幻觉之间的双向蕴含关系（创新蕴含幻觉，幻觉蕴含创新），并通过将创新率与缺失质量建立联系，推导出新的幻觉率下界。

Result: 创新是校准LLM幻觉的几乎充要条件；基于创新率得到了幻觉率的下界；通过将创新率与缺失质量关联，扩展了Kalai和Vempala关于幻觉率与缺失质量关系的结论。

Conclusion: 幻觉的根源在于模型倾向于生成训练数据中未出现的内容（创新），这一性质无法通过放弃校准来规避；创新率与缺失质量的联系为理解幻觉提供了更统一的概率框架。

Abstract: Hallucination is a central limitation of large language models (LLMs), and substantial effort has been devoted to understanding and mitigating it. Towards this, Kalai and Vempala (STOC 2024) introduced a probabilistic framework formalizing calibration and hallucination, and showed that, with high probability, calibrated LLMs hallucinate roughly at the rate of the "missing mass", a measure of how incomplete the training data is relative to its source. This raises two fundamental questions: (i) what property of a calibrated LLM makes hallucinations unavoidable? and (ii) can hallucinations be avoided by giving up calibration? We answer these questions by introducing a simpler property we call innovation that measures the tendency of a model to produce outputs outside the training data. We show that innovation is implied by the condition for hallucination identified by Kalai and Vempala, and, further, that it is an almost characterization of hallucination: hallucination implies innovation, and conversely, innovation implies hallucination with high probability. We also provide lower bounds on the hallucination rate based on the "innovation rate", and by relating innovation rate back to missing mass, we obtain new hallucination rate lower bounds based on missing mass that extend the results of Kalai and Vempala.

</details>


### [158] [The Kalman Evolve: Closing the Gap in Kalman Filtering via Interpretable Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26830)
*Vasileios Saketos,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kalman Evolve框架，通过联合优化噪声参数和更新结构（利用LLM生成可解释的非仿射修改），克服了经典卡尔曼滤波在非线性传感场景（如多普勒雷达、LiDAR）中的性能局限，在多个跟踪基准上实现了高达12%的RMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡尔曼滤波在线性动态、高斯噪声及已知协方差假设下是最优的，但在实际传感场景（如多普勒雷达、LiDAR）中这些假设常不成立，导致最优估计器本质非线性，仅调参无法消除性能差距。

Method: 提出Kalman Evolve框架，利用大语言模型(LLM)作为程序空间的先验，联合优化噪声参数和更新结构，生成保持递归形式的可解释非仿射修改，并给出仿射估计器在非线性传感模型下次优性的理论分析。

Result: 在合成和真实跟踪基准（多普勒雷达、LiDAR定位、行人跟踪）上，发现算法相比优化后的卡尔曼滤波等强基线持续改进，RMSE最多降低12%。

Conclusion: 优化卡尔曼滤波的结构（而不仅是参数）是提升状态估计性能的实用且可解释的方法。

Abstract: State estimation is a fundamental problem in control and signal processing, for which the Kalman Filter provides an optimal solution under linear dynamics, Gaussian noise, and known noise covariances. However, these assumptions often fail in realistic sensing settings such as Doppler radar and LiDAR. In these cases, the optimal estimator is inherently nonlinear, which leads to systematic performance degradation. This creates a performance gap that cannot be eliminated by tuning the noise covariance parameters (i.e., the process and measurement noise in the Kalman Filter) alone. To address this limitation, we propose Kalman Evolve, a framework for discovering improved filtering algorithms by jointly optimizing both noise parameters and the update structure. Our approach leverages large language models (LLMs) as a structured prior over program space, enabling the generation of interpretable, non-affine modifications to the classical Kalman filter while preserving its recursive form. We provide analytical results establishing the suboptimality of affine estimators under common nonlinear sensing models, motivating the need for structure-aware updates. Across a range of synthetic and real-world tracking benchmarks, including Doppler radar, LiDAR-based localization, and pedestrian tracking, the discovered algorithms consistently improve over strong baselines such as the Optimized Kalman Filter, achieving up to 12\% reduction in RMSE. These results suggest that optimizing the structure of the Kalman filter, rather than only its parameters, provides a practical and interpretable way to improve state estimation.

</details>


### [159] [MONA: Muon Optimizer with Nesterov Acceleration for Scalable Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26842)
*Jiacheng Li,Jianchao Tan,Hongtao Xu,Jiaqi Zhang,Yifan Lu,Yerui Sun,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MONA 是一种将 Muon 的正交化框架与曲率感知加速相结合的优化器，通过梯度差值的指数移动平均实现加速项，能在保持 Muon 谱范数正则化的同时逃离尖锐局部最小值，在 1B 到 68B 参数的 MoE 预训练和 SFT 中均优于 Muon 和 AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器虽在 LLM 训练中表现优异，但作为一阶方法仍会陷入尖锐局部最小值，需要一种方法在保持其正交化框架优势的同时引入曲率感知加速。

Method: MONA 在 Muon 的梯度处理流程中直接添加一个加速项，该加速项通过梯度差值的指数移动平均计算，从而在保持 Muon 谱范数正则化的同时实现曲率感知加速。

Result: 在 1B 到 68B 三种规模的 MoE 预训练（最大模型训练 1 万亿 token）中，MONA 在收敛速度和下游任务性能上均优于 Muon 和 AdamW；在 MOE-68B-A3B 模型上的 SFT 评估中，MONA 在通用能力、数学推理和代码生成基准上取得了 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: MONA 通过桥接 Muon 的正交化与曲率感知加速，有效改善了一阶优化器的局部最小值问题，在大规模 MoE 训练中展现出更优的收敛性和下游性能。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer has recently offered a promising alternative to AdamW for large language model training, leveraging matrix orthogonalization to produce geometry-aware updates. However, like all first-order methods, Muon can become trapped in sharp local minima. In this work, we present MONA, an optimizer that bridges Muon's orthogonalization framework with curvature-aware acceleration. MONA adds an acceleration term directly into Muon's gradient processing pipeline. This term is calculated from the exponential moving average of gradient differences. We provide a detailed convergence analysis for MONA, showing that the acceleration term enables escape from sharp minima while preserving Muon's spectral-norm regularization. Empirically, MONA achieves better convergence and downstream task performance compared to both Muon and AdamW across three scales of Mixture-of-Experts pretraining, spanning from 1B to 68B parameters, with the largest model trained on 1 trillion tokens. Furthermore, we conduct supervised fine-tuning on the MOE-68B-A3B model and evaluate it on general capability, mathematical reasoning, and code generation benchmarks, where MONA achieves SOTA performance.

</details>


### [160] [Learning Energy-Based Models from Stochastic Interpolants using Spatiotemporal Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26850)
*Hanlin Yu,RuiKang OuYang,Partha Kaushik,Arto Klami,Michael U. Gutmann,Omar Chehab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合时空差异的能量模型学习框架stNCE，统一了现有方法并在图像和分子数据上达到竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于时空差异的能量模型学习方法各自存在不同的失效模式，需要统一框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出Spatiotemporal Noise-Contrastive Estimation (stNCE)，通过联合时空差异来学习能量模型，统一了去噪分数匹配等现有方法。

Result: 在图像和分子数据集上的实验表明，stNCE的性能与最先进的密度估计方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: stNCE通过联合时空差异有效解决了现有方法的失效模式，并为能量模型训练提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Learning an energy-based model from data samples is a central problem in machine learning. Many recent and popular methods, such as denoising score matching for training energy-based diffusion models, use stochastic interpolants to corrupt data samples at different noise levels indexed by a time variable. This defines a joint density over both the data space and time, and most methods learn its energy through either spatial or temporal differences. We identify distinct failure modes for both of these approaches. To solve them, we propose Spatiotemporal Noise-Contrastive Estimation (stNCE), a framework for learning the energy through joint spatiotemporal differences. stNCE unifies many existing methods and leads to new training objectives. Experiments on images and molecules demonstrate performance competitive with state-of-the-art density estimation methods.

</details>


### [161] [Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection via Prototype-Based Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26857)
*Yiming Xu,Zihan Chen,Zhen Peng,Song Wang,Bin Shi,Bo Dong,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMoS是首个无监督通用图异常检测框架，通过知识蒸馏从冻结的自监督GNN教师模型向混合学生模型传递正态性先验，结合原型指导的软标签蒸馏实现跨图泛化，在零样本场景下高效检测异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用图异常检测方法依赖稀缺且昂贵的标注数据，推理时甚至需要少量样本支持，导致对多样化和未见异常模式的鲁棒性不足，亟需一种无监督、无需标注的通用检测方案。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏范式，从冻结的自监督GNN教师模型向混合学生模型（共享全局分支+轻量个性化分支）蒸馏正态性先验；提出原型指导的软标签蒸馏，在共享原型空间中对齐教师与学生，增强跨图泛化；推理时通过蒸馏偏差和原型几何偏差实现零样本异常检测。

Result: 大量实验证明了ProMoS在无标签、零样本通用图异常检测任务中的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: ProMoS为无标签、零样本的通用图异常检测提供了一条实用路径，是该领域首个无监督通用框架。

Abstract: Driven by the pressing demand for graph anomaly detection (GAD) in high-stakes domains, the generalist GAD paradigm, which trains a single detector transferable across new graphs, has recently gained growing attention. However, existing methods often rely on scarce and costly annotations for training and sometimes even require few-shot support at inference, which limits their robustness to diverse and unseen anomaly patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce ProMoS, the first unsupervised generalist GAD framework, which detects anomalies by modeling the abundant normality in unlabeled data. ProMoS adopts a knowledge-distillation paradigm to distill normality priors from a frozen self-supervised graph neural network (GNN) teacher to a mixture-of-students model with shared global and lightweight personalized branches, enabling efficient and expressive normality modeling without learning from scratch. We further propose prototype-guided soft-label distillation to align teacher and student in a shared prototype space, enhancing cross-graph generalizability. During inference, ProMoS performs zero-shot anomaly detection on unseen graphs via distillation bias and prototype geometric deviation. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness and efficiency of ProMoS, charting a practical path toward label-free, zero-shot generalist GAD.

</details>


### [162] [Negligible in Size, Significant in Effect: On Scale Vectors in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26895)
*Mingze Wang,Shuchen Zhu,Yuxin Fang,Binghui Li,Kai Shen,Shu Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM中归一化层的尺度向量（scale vectors），发现其虽参数占比极小但对预训练至关重要。理论证明在Pre-Norm架构中尺度向量通过自增强预条件作用优化后续线性映射。区分Input-Norm和Output-Norm后证明权重衰减对前者有益对后者有害。据此提出三项轻量改进：分支特异性异质性、改进的线性映射布局、幅度-方向重参数化，统一策略在0.12B至2B参数模型上持续优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管归一化层中的尺度向量被广泛使用，但其作用和原理缺乏系统性理解。本文旨在从表达能力、优化和架构结构三个角度系统研究尺度向量的作用机制，并据此提出改进方法。

Method: 实证分析移除尺度向量对预训练的影响；理论分析Pre-Norm中尺度向量的优化作用；区分Input-Norm和Output-Norm分析权重衰减的影响；提出分支特异性异质性、改进布局、幅度-方向重参数化三项改进。

Result: 移除尺度向量显著降低LLM预训练性能；理论证明尺度向量不增加表达能力但改善优化；权重衰减对Input-Norm有益而对Output-Norm有害；三项改进均带来一致提升；统一策略在0.12B-2B参数的密集和MoE模型上持续优于基线。

Conclusion: 尺度向量通过自增强预条件作用优化后续线性映射；应区分对待不同位置的归一化层与权重衰减；所提统一改进策略在几乎零参数和计算开销下持续提升LLM预训练效果。

Abstract: Normalization layers in modern large language models (LLMs) consist of a deterministic normalization operation and a learnable scale vector. While the normalization operation has been extensively studied, the scale vector remains poorly understood despite its ubiquitous use. In this work, we present a systematic study of scale vectors in LLMs from the perspectives of expressivity, optimization, and architectural structure. First, we show empirically that although scale vectors constitute only a negligible fraction of model parameters, removing them substantially degrades LLM pre-training. Our theory further shows that, in Pre-Norm architectures, scale vectors do not increase expressivity; instead, they improve optimization through a self-amplifying preconditioning effect on subsequent linear mappings. Second, we investigate the role of weight decay for scale vectors. By distinguishing Input-Norm and Output-Norm layers, we theoretically show that weight decay is beneficial for the former but harmful for the latter, due to their distinct roles in optimization and expressivity. Third, motivated by this understanding, we propose three lightweight and complementary improvements to scale vectors: branch-specific heterogeneity, improved placement around linear mappings, and magnitude-direction reparameterization. Both theory and experiments show that each improvement yields consistent gains. Finally, we combine these improvements into a unified scale-vector strategy and evaluate it through extensive LLM pre-training experiments on dense and mixture-of-experts models ranging from 0.12B to 2B parameters, across multiple optimizers and learning rate schedules, under industrial-scale token budgets. The unified strategy consistently achieves lower terminal loss than well-tuned baselines and exhibits more favorable scaling behavior, while adding negligible parameter and computational overhead.

</details>


### [163] [SPHERE-JEPA: Spherical Prediction with Homogeneous Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26900)
*Léo Nicollier,Max Dunitz,Marc Pic,Pablo Musé,Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在流形上自监督学习的最优表征几何是超球面均匀分布而非高斯分布，并据此提出SPHERE-JEPA框架，在纹理检索和ImageNet-1K线性探针上分别提升6%和1.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeJEPA在欧氏空间中提出各向同性高斯嵌入最优，但该结论未覆盖低维流形（如超球面）上的分布。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，并揭示高斯嵌入在实际应用中的局限性。

Method: 1) 在黎曼流形上进行极小极大分析，证明均匀分布对k近邻最优，球面均匀分布对指数点积核和线性核的核岭回归最优；2) 基于该理论，修改LeJEPA的Cramér-Wold投影机制，强制学习超球面均匀分布而非高斯先验，提出SPHERE-JEPA。

Result: SPHERE-JEPA在纹理检索mAP上提升超过6%，在ImageNet-1K线性探针（ViT-B/14）上提升1.8%，其他标准基准上持平或优于LeJEPA。

Conclusion: 自监督学习的表征几何最优解取决于数据流形结构。高斯嵌入在流形场景下存在各向异性偏差，而超球面均匀分布能有效消除该偏差，提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: A fundamental open question in self-supervised learning (SSL) is the explicit characterization of the optimal geometry of the learned representations. Recently, LeJEPA identified isotropic Gaussian embeddings as optimal for minimizing downstream prediction risk in Euclidean spaces. However, the corresponding problem for distributions supported on lower-dimensional manifolds, such as the hypersphere, remains unexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that extending this minimax analysis to smooth distributions on Riemannian manifolds fundamentally changes the optimal solution. We show that, under a worst-case formulation, both k-nearest neighbors and kernel ridge regression induce hyperspherical uniformity. More precisely, we show that uniform distributions on manifolds are optimal for k-nearest neighbors, and that the uniform distribution on the sphere is optimal for kernel ridge regression with both the exponential dot-product kernel and the linear kernel. This theoretical insight reveals a fundamental limitation of Gaussian embeddings: their non-uniform density induces anisotropic k-NN neighborhoods, severely biasing the estimator. To correct this, we introduce SPHERE-JEPA, a theoretically grounded SSL framework. We adapt LeJEPA's Cram{é}r-Wold projection mechanism to enforce hyperspherical uniformity rather than a Gaussian prior. Empirically, SPHERE-JEPA yields significant improvements, boosting texture retrieval mAP by over 6%, while consistently matching or outperforming LeJEPA on standard benchmarks-including a +1.8% linear probing gain on ImageNet-1K (ViT-B/14).

</details>


### [164] [EEG-FM-Audit: A Systematic Evaluation and Analysis Pipeline for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26910)
*Xianheng Wang,Yige Yang,Damien Coyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EEG-FM-Audit评估框架，系统化评估大型脑电图基础模型。通过对四个SOTA EEG-FM和五个监督模型的实验发现：适当调优的监督基线可以用更少参数匹敌或超越先进FM；FM的学习范式有效性高度依赖数据集规模和架构；神经生理探测揭示FM依赖特定的生理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型研究存在三个关键局限：监督基线调优不透明、复杂学习范式的贡献未经验证、模型决策缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出EEG-FM-Audit评估分析流程，包含三个组件：(1) ASHA驱动的基准测试协议，透明优化监督基线；(2) 范式级消融实验，评估学习范式的有效性；(3) 神经生理探测(NPP)框架，探究FM是否利用了有效的时空频谱EEG特征。

Result: 适当调优的监督基线可以匹敌或超越先进FM但参数更少；FM学习范式的有效性高度依赖数据集规模和架构；NPP分析揭示了FM依赖特定的生理特征。

Conclusion: 提出了更透明、可解释的EEG-FM评估框架，为神经解码的可解释性建立了基础。

Abstract: Large EEG Foundation Models (FMs) have shown great potential for decoding EEG signals across diverse cognitive tasks. However, existing EEG-FM studies exhibit three critical limitations: opaque supervised baseline tuning, unverified contributions of complex learning paradigms, and a lack of transparency in model decision-making. To address these, we propose EEG-FM-Audit, a comprehensive evaluation and analysis pipeline designed to systematize the assessment of EEG-FMs. EEG-FM-Audit consists of three primary components: (1) an ASHA-driven benchmarking protocol that ensures fair comparisons by transparently optimizing supervised baselines; (2) paradigm-level ablation studies to evaluate the effectiveness of learning paradigms in FMs; and (3) a neurophysiological probing (NPP) framework, which explores whether FMs leverage valid temporal, spatial, and spectral EEG properties. We apply EEG-FM-Audit to four state-of-the-art EEG-FMs and five representative supervised models across three public datasets. Our results reveal that properly tuned supervised baselines can match or outperform advanced FMs, despite requiring significantly fewer parameters. Furthermore, we find that the effectiveness of learning paradigms of FMs is highly dependent on dataset scale and architecture. Finally, NPP analysis demonstrates how FMs rely on specific physiological features, establishing a framework for more interpretable neural decoding.

</details>


### [165] [Agile Online Model Selection: Resolving Adaptation Lag via Safeguarded Large Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26919)
*Kei Takemura,Ryuta Matsuno,Keita Sakuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带有事后惩罚机制的乐观在线镜像下降算法，通过使用高达Θ(T)的大学习率来消除自适应滞后，同时利用O(log T)的累积惩罚保证近最优的动态遗憾界，在非平稳环境中实现了鲁棒性与敏捷性的兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免调参算法在非平稳环境中面临鲁棒性与敏捷性的根本性权衡：为保证动态遗憾界，被迫将学习率限制为小常数（如O(1)），导致在分布突变时产生显著的自适应滞后。

Method: 提出一种乐观在线镜像下降（Optimistic Online Mirror Descent）方法，核心创新是事后惩罚（post-hoc penalty）机制——动态监控不稳定更新，排除产生过大遗憾的学习率，从而允许使用高达Θ(T)的大学习率而无需先验约束。

Result: 累积惩罚仅为O(log T)，算法在最坏情况下达到近最优保证，在良性情况下获得更优速率。实验表明自适应滞后从数百轮降至数轮，在合成数据和11个真实数据集上持续优于免调参基线。

Conclusion: 通过事后惩罚机制成功解耦了学习率与动态遗憾界之间的刚性约束，在保持理论保证的同时大幅提升了模型对分布突变的响应速度。

Abstract: Maintaining predictive accuracy in non-stationary environments requires online model selection to adapt autonomously to unknown distribution shifts. However, existing tuning-free algorithms face a fundamental trade-off between robustness and agility. Specifically, to ensure dynamic regret bounds, they must restrict learning rates to small constants (e.g., $O(1)$). This restriction inevitably causes significant adaptation lag during abrupt changes. To resolve this, we propose a novel optimistic online mirror descent that utilizes safeguarded large learning rates up to $Θ(T)$, where $T$ is the number of rounds. Our key technical contribution is a post-hoc penalty mechanism that dynamically monitors unstable updates and excludes learning rates incurring excessive regret, eliminating the need for restrictive a priori constraints. We show that the cumulative penalty remains $O(\log T)$, allowing our algorithm to match near-optimal worst-case guarantees while achieving superior rates in benign cases. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and eleven diverse real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach reduces the adaptation lag from hundreds of rounds to a few rounds, consistently outperforming tuning-free baselines.

</details>


### [166] [When Muon Optimizer Meets Adversarial Training: A Theoretical and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26929)
*Jun Yan,Weiquan Huang,Jiankai Zuo,Yujian Mo,Xi Fang,Chengliang Wu,Zeming Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了正交化优化器（Muon）在对抗训练中的表现。理论证明Muon对矩阵更新施加谱范数稳定性上限，限制训练动态中不可控的谱增长；实验表明在五种架构和三种威胁模型下，Muon在CNN上与SGD竞争力相当，在CNN和ViT上均显著优于AdamW。研究表明优化器设计是对抗训练的安全关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器（如AdamW）在标准训练中表现优异但在对抗训练中鲁棒性较差，而SGD虽常被使用但非最优。近期Muon优化器在大规模训练中取得显著成功且内存成本与SGD相当，这激发了研究正交化优化是否能提升对抗训练在强异质威胁模型下的鲁棒性。

Method: 理论上分析了Muon优化器对矩阵更新施加谱范数稳定性上限的机制；实验上在五种架构（CNN和ViT）和三种ℓ_p威胁模型（ℓ_∞, ℓ_1, ℓ_2）及其联合威胁下，对比Muon、SGD和AdamW的对抗训练效果。

Result: Muon在CNN上对抗训练效果与SGD竞争力相当，在CNN和ViT上均显著优于AdamW；Muon能够限制训练动态中矩阵更新的不可控谱增长，而不会显式收缩学习到的权重。

Conclusion: 优化器的几何特性是对抗训练的安全相关因素，正交化更新在特定经验场景下具有优势；优化器设计应被视为对抗训练的安全关键组件。

Abstract: Adversarial training (AT) remains one of the most reliable empirical defenses against adversarial attacks. Its robustness critically depends on how the underlying min-max objective is optimized. In practice, Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) optimizer remains the default optimization choice for AT, whereas adaptive optimizers often improve standard training but may yield inferior robustness. Recently, the Muon optimizer, which orthogonalizes matrix-valued updates via an approximate polar decomposition, has achieved notable success in large-scale training at a memory cost comparable to SGD. This raises a security-relevant question: \textit{can orthogonalized optimization improve AT under strong and heterogeneous threat models?} Focusing on this problem, we conduct a comprehensive theoretical and empirical study. Theoretically, we show that Muon imposes a spectral-norm stability ceiling on matrix updates, limiting uncontrolled spectral growth in the training dynamics without explicitly shrinking the learned weights. Empirically, across five architectures and three $\ell_p$ threat models ($\ell_\infty$, $\ell_1$, $\ell_2$) and their union, Muon is competitive with SGD on CNNs and substantially outperforms AdamW on both CNNs and ViTs. These results identify optimizer geometry as a security-relevant factor in adversarial training, while clarifying the empirical regimes in which orthogonalized updates are beneficial. Overall, our findings highlight optimizer design as a security-critical component of AT.

</details>


### [167] [RLVR Datasets and Where to Find Them: Tracing Data Lineage for Better Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26971)
*Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Weijie Liu,Chenming Tang,Sanwoo Lee,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS框架，系统追溯RLVR数据集的血缘关系，发现99.7%的实例可归因于20个原子来源，并基于此构建去污染数据集DAPO++，提出源级反事实归因（SCA）原则和复合质量分数Q，有效预测和提升下游RLVR训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR数据集的无序扩散导致来源崩溃（provenance collapse），现有数据集之间血缘关系不清晰，缺乏系统的溯源框架。

Method: 提出ATLAS（Atomic-source Tracing via Lineage-Aware Search）框架进行血缘感知的原子源溯源；提出源级反事实归因（SCA）原则，通过比较基于各原子源的RL检查点与共享基线的边际效用来衡量样本效用；基于SCA设计复合质量分数Q。

Result: ATLAS将145万实例中99.7%归因到20个原子源；大多数RLVR数据集是少数共享上游来源的变体；DAPO++在Qwen3系列模型上持续提升保留基准性能；Q与下游RLVR训练效果强相关。

Conclusion: 血缘感知的溯源框架和去污染数据构建策略能有效提升RLVR训练效果，复合质量分数Q可可靠预测训练有效性。

Abstract: The proliferation of Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) datasets has exacerbated provenance collapse due to unclear lineage among existing datasets. To bridge this fragmented RLVR data landscape, we propose Atomic-source Tracing via Lineage-Aware Search (ATLAS), a systematic framework for tracing RLVR datasets back to their atomic sources, attributing over 99.7% of 1.45M instances to 20 atomic sources. Our analysis reveals that most RLVR datasets are variants of a small set of shared upstream sources, with few introducing genuinely new data, and many facing data contamination risks. These findings naturally motivate us to curate a new RLVR dataset, DAPO++, and to benchmark existing datasets from a lineage-aware perspective. To this end, we propose Source-level Counterfactual Attribution (SCA) as a guiding principle to curate a decontaminated training dataset with concentrated learning signals. Essentially, SCA measures a sample's marginal utility by comparing per-atomic-source RL checkpoints against a shared base model. Building upon these attribution signals, we further design a composite dataset quality score Q that strongly correlates with downstream RLVR performance. Experiments on Qwen3 series models verify that DAPO++ consistently improves performance on held-out benchmarks, while Q reliably predicts downstream RLVR training effectiveness. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/Celine-hxy/ATLAS.

</details>


### [168] [Convergence of Spectral Descent for Non-smooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26977)
*Yixuan Yang,Yuqing He,Song Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器在非光滑凸优化下的理论收敛性，通过分析其简化变体Spectral Descent (SD)和Truncated Spectral Descent (TSD)，建立了全局线性收敛保证，并拓展到含解耦权重衰减的正则化变体，最后在鲁棒低秩矩阵恢复任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在训练大语言模型中表现出色，但其理论机制尚不清晰，尤其是非光滑收敛行为缺乏研究。现有收敛性分析过度依赖光滑性假设，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出Spectral Descent (SD)及其截断版本Truncated Spectral Descent (TSD)作为Muon的简化变体；在凸性、Lipschitz连续性和sharpness条件下建立非光滑凸优化的全局线性收敛；通过Frank-Wolfe方法关联分析含解耦权重衰减的正则化变体并推导次线性收敛界；应用于鲁棒低秩矩阵恢复的混合稀疏与稠密噪声场景。

Result: 在非光滑凸设定下，SD和TSD均实现全局线性收敛；正则化变体获得次线性收敛保证；在鲁棒低秩矩阵恢复中给出严格的恢复保证；数值实验验证了理论结果及Muon类方法在非光滑优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统建立了Muon类方法在非光滑凸优化下的收敛理论，拓展了其应用范围，为理解Muon优化器的工作机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer has recently demonstrated remarkable empirical success in training large language models. However, the theoretical understanding of its mechanisms remains limited. Current convergence guarantees for Muon rely heavily on smoothness assumptions, leaving its non-smooth convergence behavior largely unexplored. In this work, we take a step toward bridging this gap by investigating Spectral Descent (SD), a simplified variant of Muon, together with its truncated counterpart, Truncated Spectral Descent (TSD). Under convexity, Lipschitz continuity, and sharpness conditions, we establish global linear convergence for both SD and TSD in non-smooth convex formulations. We also study regularized variants equipped with decoupled weight decay and derive sublinear convergence guarantees through their connection with Frank-Wolfe methods. Finally, we apply our theoretical framework to robust low-rank matrix recovery under mixed sparse and dense noise regimes and provide rigorous recovery guarantees. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of Muon-type methods for non-smooth optimization.

</details>


### [169] [Sampling Data with Chains of Forward-Backward Diffusion Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27006)
*Hyunmo Kang,Noam Itzhak Levi,Corinna Elena Wegner,Daniel J. Korchinski,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出U-turn链，一种基于扩散模型短前后步迭代的马尔可夫链，用于从高维分布中采样。研究发现，最小U-turn动力学存在遍历性破缺相变，由数据流形碎片化驱动；在非遍历区，低层特征比高层特征松弛更快，仅在大U-turn幅度下顺序反转。该现象在自然语言和图像中均存在，讨论了其对扩散模型采样的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从学习到的高维分布中进行采样是一个基础性计算问题，现有方法在保持数据流形结构的同时实现高效采样仍面临挑战，特别是遍历性破缺和特征松弛顺序问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 提出U-turn链：通过迭代扩散模型的短前向-后向步骤构建马尔可夫链，每一步提出保持在学得数据流形上的移动，并配合Metropolis-Hastings校正从能量修正目标中采样。

Result: 在合成语言中观察到遍历性破缺相变；非遍历区低层特征先于高层特征松弛，大U-turn幅度下顺序反转。在自然语言和自然图像中，最小U-turn松弛缓慢，尤其对CNN或LLM深层表示的高层特征，层序反转仅在大噪声（高效混合）时出现。

Conclusion: 这些现象与强约束、弱混合的局部动力学一致，对扩散模型采样策略设计有重要启示。

Abstract: Sampling from learned high-dimensional distributions is a foundational computational problem. We introduce U-turn chains: Markov chains obtained by iterating short forward-backward steps of a diffusion model, in which each step proposes a move that remains on the learned data manifold and, paired with a Metropolis-Hastings correction, samples from energy-modified targets. For synthetic languages, we show that minimal U-turn dynamics undergoes an ergodicity-breaking phase transition driven by fragmentation of the data manifold; ergodicity is restored at larger U-turn magnitude. In the non-ergodic regime, low-level features relax faster than high-level ones, an ordering that inverts only at sufficiently large U-turn magnitude. We test these predictions on natural language and natural images. In both modalities, minimal U-turns relax slowly, especially for high-level features approximated by deep representations in CNNs or LLMs. The layer-ordering inversion appears only at large noise when mixing is efficient -- signatures consistent with strongly constrained, weakly mixing local dynamics. We discuss the implications of these results for sampling with diffusion models.

</details>


### [170] [SCENT: Aligning Mass Spectra with Molecular Structure for Olfactory Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27009)
*Ziqi Zhang,Eunyeong Jin,Miguel Vasco,Farzaneh Taleb,Nona Rajabi,Alexandra Gutmann,Jonathan Williams,Antônio H. Ribeiro,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCENT框架，通过对比学习将电子电离质谱（EI-MS）表示与预训练的化学结构嵌入对齐，使得在推理时仅需质谱即可实现与基于结构模型相当的气味预测性能，无需显式化学结构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于化学结构的气味预测方法在推理时需要显式的分子结构信息，而实际传感场景中这类信息通常不可获得。因此需要探索可直接获取的质谱数据作为替代输入模态进行嗅觉预测。

Method: 提出SCENT（Spectrum-to-Chemical Embedding alignmeNT），一种多模态对比学习框架，通过将EI-MS表示与预训练的化学结构嵌入进行跨模态对齐，在推理时仅需质谱数据即可进行气味预测。

Result: 在多标签气味描述词预测任务中，SCENT显著优于仅使用质谱的基线方法，性能与基于结构的模型相当；学到的表示能更好逼近连续的人类感知评分，并泛化到真实实验室测量的质谱数据。

Conclusion: 跨模态对齐是将分析质谱数据锚定到化学语义上的有效策略，能够在不依赖显式分子结构的情况下实现高质量的气味感知预测。

Abstract: Predicting human olfactory perception from molecular structure has seen remarkable progress, yet these approaches require explicit chemical structure at inference, which is not available in practical sensing settings. We address this gap by exploring direct electron ionization mass spectrometry (EI-MS), a sensing technique that acquires chemically informative fragmentation fingerprints in seconds, as an alternative input modality for olfactory prediction. We contribute Spectrum-to-Chemical Embedding alignmeNT (SCENT), a multi-modal contrastive learning framework that aligns EI-MS representations with pretrained chemical structure embeddings, while requiring only mass spectra at inference. On the multi-label odor descriptor prediction task, SCENT significantly outperforms MS-only baselines and achieves performance comparable to structure-based models, despite requiring no explicit molecular structure at test time. The learned representations also better approximate continuous human perceptual ratings and generalize to real-world lab-measured spectra, suggesting that cross-modal alignment is an effective strategy for grounding analytical spectra in chemical semantics.

</details>


### [171] [SQARL: A Size-Agnostic Reinforcement Learning approach for Circuit Allocation in Distributed Quantum Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27027)
*Víctor Carballo,Júlia López-Closa,Mario Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的灵活分布式量子计算架构，在处理量子比特分配问题上，无需针对不同硬件配置重新训练，并在分配成本上相比最先进的启发式方法HQA实现了33%（Cuccaro Adder）和25%（随机电路）的显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式量子计算中的量子比特分配问题主要依赖启发式算法（如HQA），而强化学习方法虽能学习分配策略，但缺乏对硬件拓扑的灵活性，需要随硬件配置变化重新训练，且解的质量仍不及非学习方法。

Method: 提出一种基于Transformer的灵活架构，能够处理任意数量的量子比特和核心，无需重新训练。通过端到端学习来优化跨核心通信成本。

Result: 该策略在分配成本上相比之前最先进的RL方法有持续提升，并缩小了与HQA的差距。对于Cuccaro Adder实现33%的成本降低，随机电路平均降低25%。

Conclusion: 学习型方法能够有效匹配手工启发式算法的性能，这是向实际应用部署迈出的关键一步。

Abstract: The scaling of quantum processors is currently limited by technical challenges such as decoherence and cross-talk. As the number of qubits grows, interference increases the computational noise. Distributed quantum computing addresses these limitations by interconnecting smaller, easier-to-handle quantum processors (cores), but it introduces the challenge of minimizing slow, error-prone inter-core communication. The task of distributing quantum circuits across cores while minimizing communication costs is known as the Qubit Allocation problem. This work focuses on developing a deep learning approach to this problem, emphasizing flexibility to quantum hardware topology and improving state-of-the-art performance.
  Heuristic and non-learning algorithms, such as the Hungarian Qubit Allocation (HQA), currently represent the state of the art. Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches leverage learned allocation policies but often lack flexibility, requiring retraining when hardware configurations change, and they fall short of the solution quality achieved by non-learning methods. However, learning mechanisms could outperform human-crafted heuristics.
  To overcome these limitations, this work proposes a flexible, transformer-based architecture that can handle arbitrary numbers of qubits and cores without retraining. Results show that the trained policy consistently outperforms the previous RL state of the art and narrows the gap between RL and HQA for the most common circuits. It achieves a 33% reduction in allocation cost relative to the HQA for the Cuccaro Adder and 25% on average for random circuits. These findings show that learning-based approaches can effectively match the performance of hand-crafted heuristics, a crucial step towards their application in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [172] [Less is More: Early Stopping Rollout for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27028)
*Zhou Ziheng,Jiaqi Li,Huacong Tang,Ying Nian Wu,Demetri Terzopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出早期停止 rollout (ESR) 策略来解决 on-policy 蒸馏中的"离策略教师衰退"问题，即限制 rollout 生成仅在前 K 个 token 上，从而提升性能、GPU 效率和训练稳定性，甚至在某些情况下超越教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 on-policy 蒸馏中，标准做法是让学生模型生成完整序列并用教师模型评分。但研究发现存在"离策略教师衰退"问题：对于序列靠后的 token，由于学生模型早期轨迹与教师策略分布不一致（off-policy），教师模型产生修正性评分的能力会下降，退化为预训练阶段的 token 补全行为。这限制了大语言模型蒸馏效果的提升。

Method: 提出 Early Stopping Rollout (ESR) 策略：在 on-policy 蒸馏训练时，限制学生模型的 rollout 生成仅在前 K 个 response token 处停止。通过这种简单的位置选择策略，避免了后期 token 因上下文 off-policy 导致的教师评分退化问题。

Result: ESR 在多种模型规模、模型家族、任务类型和训练范式下，均超越完整 rollout 的 on-policy 蒸馏性能。同时展现出更高的 GPU 利用效率和训练稳定性，特别是在跨模型家族场景下优势更明显。发现了"级联对齐"和"子模态承诺"两种效应来解释其有效性，甚至能超越教师模型性能。

Conclusion: 基于位置的 token 选择策略（ESR）不能完全被 KL 散度和熵信号所解释，揭示了 on-policy 蒸馏中存在未被充分研究的离策略问题，为 LLM 蒸馏提供了更高效、稳定的训练范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation has recently emerged as a promising alternative to standard sequence-level imitation, training a student by scoring its own rollouts with a teacher model. However, we observe ``Off-policy Teacher Decay'' problem in this paradigm: for the later tokens, with student's earlier trajectory as context that is off-policy to the teacher, the teacher's ability to produce a corrective score would decay, and may fall back to token-completion behavior learned in the pre-training stage. We empirically verify this problem, and we propose Early Stopping Rollout (ESR) to fix it: a simple yet effective distillation strategy that simply restricts the rollout generation to the first
  response tokens. We show that ESR both surpasses the full rollout OPD performance across model size, family, tasks and training regime, and exhibit much higher GPU efficiency and training stability, especially under cross model family scenarios. We further investigate the mechanism behind this surprising performance and discovered "Cascading Alignment" and "Sub-mode Commitment" effect of ESR that may explain why it works effectively and even sometimes exceeding the teacher model performance. Besides, we show that this position-based token selection strategy cannot be fully explainable by KL divergence and entropy signals.

</details>


### [173] [Learning to Orchestrate Agents under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27073)
*Mary Chriselda Antony Oliver,Lan Jiang,Aaron Bundi Anampiu,Elaf Almahmoud,Francesco Quinzan,Umang Bhatt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BOT-Orch框架，将异构智能体自适应编排建模为正则化bandit问题，通过最优传输距离正则化实现不确定性下的任务分配，理论上证明O(√T)遗憾界，实验优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体编排方法仅关注性能或成本，未显式建模智能体可靠性和输出分布的不确定性，导致在异构、非平稳场景下决策鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将编排问题转化为bandit问题，用最优传输(OT)距离度量智能体输出分布与任务参考分布的对齐程度，以此作为正则化项指导元控制器的委派决策。

Result: 理论：在标准假设下达到O(√T)遗憾界，能对均值相同但分布对齐不同的智能体诱导偏好排序。实验：在合成对抗性任务分配中优于标准bandit和启发式基线。

Conclusion: BOT-Orch通过分布对齐正则化有效处理了异构智能体编排中的不确定性问题，为自适应委派决策提供了理论保证和实用框架。

Abstract: Adaptive orchestration of heterogeneous agents requires making sequential delegation decisions under uncertain and evolving agent behaviour, e.g., coordinating specialised AI models with varying reliability, cost, and response quality. While prior work on agent orchestration focuses on performance or cost, uncertainty in agent reliability and output distributions is typically not modelled explicitly at the orchestration level. In this work, we study the problem of adaptive orchestration of heterogeneous agents under uncertainty, where a meta-controller must decide when to delegate to an agent, accounting for reliability, cost, and uncertainty. We propose BOT-Orch, a lightweight framework that recasts orchestration as a bandit problem over agents, regularized by OT distances between agent output distributions and task-specific reference distributions. We show that the regularised orchestration enjoys $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under standard assumptions, and provably induces preference ordering among agents with identical mean rewards but differing distributional alignment. Empirically, we demonstrate that BOT-Orch outperforms standard bandit and heuristic baselines in synthetic but adversarial task allocation settings with heterogeneous, non-i.i.d. agent behaviour.

</details>


### [174] [Two Speeds of Learning: A Representation-Readout Decomposition of Grokking and Double Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27078)
*Chi-Ning Chou,Oscar Uzdelewicz,Neng-Chun Chiu,Yao-Yuan Yang,SueYeon Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"表征-读出分解"框架，将深度学习训练动力学分解为编码器表征学习和最终分类器读出校准两个竞争过程。通过表征几何、神经正切核和线性探测等工具，该框架成功解释了grokking（延迟泛化）和epoch-wise double descent（非单调泛化）等看似反常的泛化现象，揭示了这些现象源于两个过程相对速度的波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对grokking和epoch-wise double descent等反常泛化现象的解释往往是任务特定的，缺乏一个跨任务和架构的通用分析框架。作者希望建立一个任务无关的分析框架来诊断和解释这些现象。

Method: 提出表征-读出分解框架，利用表征几何、神经正切核和线性探测等工具，将学习动力学分解为编码器表征学习和分类器读出校准两个竞争过程，并分析它们的相对速度波动。

Result: 1. 发现grokking中读出层在泛化发生前偏向训练集，表征学习是渐进的但并未缺失（反驳了lazy-to-rich假说）；2. 在MNIST grokking和epoch-wise double descent例子中，发现非标准训练配方导致的表征退化和读出错位是延迟或非单调泛化的根源；3. 提供了区分虚假泛化和真正泛化的诊断特征。

Conclusion: 表征-读出分解是一个有效的自上而下框架，可用于理解学习动力学并揭示底层算法，对可解释性研究有重要意义。

Abstract: Training loss and accuracy are the standard signals used to monitor generalization during deep neural network training. Two well-documented phenomena complicate this picture: in grokking, train loss falls rapidly while test performance improves abruptly only after a long delay; in epoch-wise double descent, train loss decreases monotonically while test loss or error rises and falls. Existing accounts are often task-specific, and a task-agnostic analysis framework for diagnosing and explaining these phenomena across realistic tasks and architectures is missing. We address this challenge by analyzing two competing processes that underlie learning dynamics: representation learning in the encoder and readout calibration in the final classifier. Using tools from representational geometry, neural tangent kernels, and linear probing, we show that both processes are active throughout training, with the fluctuations of their relative speed giving rise to seemingly anomalous generalization dynamics. Applying the representation-readout decomposition to grokking across a wide range of tasks and architectures, we find that the readout is train-biased before grokking onset, and representation learning is gradual but not absent, contrary to the lazy-to-rich account. The framework further provides diagnostic signatures distinguishing spurious from genuine generalization: in a previously reported MNIST grokking example and an epoch-wise double descent example, apparent delayed or non-monotone generalization is shown to arise from representation degradation and readout misalignment induced by non-standard training recipes. Together, these results establish the representation-readout decomposition as a top-down framework for understanding learning dynamics and revealing underlying algorithms for interpretability research.

</details>


### [175] [Trust Region Q Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27079)
*Yonghoon Dong,Kyungmin Lee,Changyeon Kim,Jaehyuk Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRQAM算法，通过投影对偶下降自适应控制路径空间KL散度，解决预训练流策略的离策略强化学习中的优化不稳定问题，在50个OGBench任务中离线RL成功率达68%，远超最强基线46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAM方法中批评家引导的改进存在固有脆弱性：当批评家条件不佳时，微小的批评家误差会被放大，常导致模型崩溃。需要一种更稳定的离策略微调算法。

Method: 提出Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM)，通过投影对偶下降自适应控制与预训练流策略之间的路径空间KL散度。优化SOC动力学中的信任区域参数λ，并理论上证明路径空间KL可表示为λ的闭式函数，从而精确控制与预训练策略的偏差。

Result: 在50个OGBench任务上，TRQAM在离线RL和离线到在线RL中均持续优于先前方法。离线RL总体成功率达68%，大幅超越最强基线46%。

Conclusion: TRQAM通过自适应信任区域机制实现了稳定的离策略强化学习，有效解决了批评家引导优化的不稳定性问题。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning of pretrained flow policies remains challenging due to the instability of optimization arising from the multi-step sampling process. Recently, Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) addressed this issue by reformulating into a memoryless stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem with a learned critic. However, QAM inherits a fundamental fragility of critic-guided improvement: small critic errors are amplified when critics are ill-conditioned, often leading to model collapse. This paper introduces Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM), a stable off-policy fine-tuning algorithm that adaptively controls the path-space KL with pretrained flow policies through projected dual descent. Specifically, we optimize the trust-region parameter $λ$ in SOC dynamics, and theoretically show that the path-space KL can be represented by a closed-form function of $λ$. As a result, our method can precisely control the exact deviation from pretrained flow policies, achieving stable off-policy RL. Through experiments on 50 OGBench tasks, TRQAM consistently outperforms prior arts in both offline RL and offline-to-online RL. In particular, TRQAM achieves an overall success rate of 68% in offline RL, substantially improves the strongest baseline at 46%.

</details>


### [176] [ReMoE: Boosting Expert Reuse through Router Fine-Tuning in Memory-Constrained MoE LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27081)
*Xiongwei Zhu,Xiaojian Liao,Tianyang Jiang,Yusen Zhang,Liang Wang,Limin Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReMoE通过微调MoE路由策略，使路由倾向于最近选中的专家，提升时序稳定性，在不增加推理计算的前提下减少专家缓存未命中次数，实验表明专家复用率提升26%，吞吐量最高提升8.4%，解码速度提升1.77-1.99倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存受限的推理场景中，MoE模型只能缓存少量专家，未命中专家需从慢速存储（如UFS）加载，导致频繁的缓存逐出和巨大的I/O开销。

Method: 提出ReMoE框架，通过对路由器进行微调，使其偏向于最近被选中的专家，产生时序上更稳定的路由策略，从而更好地匹配缓存局部性约束，增加短时专家复用率。

Result: 在DeepSeek和Qwen模型上，专家复用率提升26%；vLLM GPU-CPU专家卸载下输出吞吐量提升8.4%；llama.cpp在Jetson Orin NX上TPOT降低43.6-49.8%，解码速度提升1.77-1.99倍。

Conclusion: ReMoE在不增加推理计算开销的前提下，通过路由器微调有效提升专家时序复用率，显著降低I/O开销，是一种轻量且高效的MoE推理加速方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models sparsely activate only a subset of experts per token, reducing activated computation while maintaining high model capacity. However, in memory-constrained inference scenarios, only a small set of experts can be cached. Experts not in the cache must be fetched from slow external storage (e.g., UFS), leading to frequent evictions and substantial I/O overhead.
  We propose ReMoE, a router fine-tuning framework designed to boost token-wise expert reuse. ReMoE biases the router toward recently selected experts, producing temporally stable routing that better matches cache locality constraints. By increasing short-horizon expert reuse, ReMoE reduces expert fetches from storage without adding inference-time computation.
  Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen models show that ReMoE improves expert reuse by 26% while maintaining downstream task performance. Real-system evaluations further confirm these benefits, improving output throughput by 8.4% under vLLM GPU-CPU expert offloading and reducing TPOT by 43.6-49.8% under llama.cpp on Jetson Orin NX, corresponding to a 1.77-1.99$\times$ decode speedup across diverse workloads. Checkpoints and usage instructions are available at https://github.com/BUAA-OSCAR/ReMoE.

</details>


### [177] [Adversarial Dual On-Policy Distillation from Expressive Flow-based Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27095)
*Zhenglin Wan,Jingxuan Wu,Xingrui Yu,Chubin Zhang,Mingcong Lei,Bo An,Ivor W. Tsang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FA-OPD方法，通过对抗式双通道在线策略蒸馏，将流匹配教师模型与轻量级MLP学生模型联合训练，利用奖励通道和动作通道分别提供探索信号和局部监督，在机器人导航、操作和运动六个基准上优于强基线方法，并在噪声或有限演示下表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行为克隆和扩散/流匹配策略仅作为离线监督学习者，策略仅训练于专家状态，无法获得访问状态的矫正信号；而标准在线策略蒸馏依赖强固定教师，这在仅含演示的控制场景中不可得。

Method: 提出FA-OPD，一种对抗式双在线策略蒸馏方法。从演示中学习流匹配（FM）教师，与轻量级MLP学生联合训练。教师提供两个互补信号：奖励通道学习状态-动作对的专家似然目标，驱动长程策略优化的在线探索；动作通道在学生访问状态提供密集局部目标，稳定利用。两者耦合使奖励蒸馏超越逐点演示实现泛化，动作蒸馏将探索锚定在专家行为附近。

Result: 在六个机器人导航、操作和运动基准上，FA-OPD击败强基线方法，且在噪声或有限演示下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FA-OPD通过对抗式双通道在线策略蒸馏有效解决了演示控制中策略无法获得状态矫正信号的问题，提升了泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations in embodied control is often cast as behavioral cloning, and recent diffusion or flow-matching policies improve this paradigm by modeling multi-modal expert actions. Yet these methods remain offline supervised learners: the policy is trained only on expert states and receives no corrective signal on the states it actually visits. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a natural remedy, but standard OPD assumes a strong fixed teacher, which is unavailable in demonstration-only control. We propose \textbf{FA-OPD}, an \emph{adversarial dual on-policy distillation} method in which a Flow Matching (FM) teacher is learned from demonstrations and co-trained with a lightweight MLP student. The teacher provides two complementary signals on student rollouts. The reward channel learns an expert-likeness objective over state-action pairs and drives online exploration through long-horizon policy optimization. The action channel supplies dense local targets at student-visited states, stabilizing exploitation. FA-OPD couples them so that reward distillation enables generalization beyond point-wise demonstrations, while action distillation keeps exploration anchored near expert-like behavior. Across six robot navigation, manipulation, and locomotion benchmarks, FA-OPD beats strong baselines and shows much stronger robustness under noisy or limited demonstrations.

</details>


### [178] [DEI: Diversity in Evolutionary Inference for Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27130)
*John Donaghy,Shikhar Rastogi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DEI框架，利用异构大语言模型作为变异算子在分布式QD搜索中协同工作，在Core War领域取得显著性能提升，证明模型多样性而非单纯并行化是分布式LLM-based QD搜索增益的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同质化并行搜索中单一模型的归纳偏差限制了行为新颖性；异构LLM的创造性先验可作为互补的新颖性来源，但缺乏有效整合框架。

Method: 提出DEI（Diversity in Evolutionary Inference）框架，将异构LLM作为变异算子分布在节点间，节点间通过非阻塞集合通信共享局部最优解，形成跨模型对抗压力驱动鲁棒性提升。

Result: 四节点异构集成在QD-Score上提升124%（45.90 vs 20.46），覆盖率达80.6%（vs 63.0%），且在所有四种模型系列上均优于同质集成。

Conclusion: 模型多样性是分布式LLM-based QD搜索增益的关键驱动因素，而不仅仅是并行化。

Abstract: We present DEI: Diversity in Evolutionary Inference, a distributed Quality-Diversity (QD) search framework that assigns heterogeneous large language models (LLMs) as mutation operators across peer nodes communicating with non-blocking collective operations. Unlike homogeneous parallel search, which replicates a single model's inductive biases across all workers, DEI treats each LLM's distinct creative prior as a complementary source of behavioral novelty. Extending the Digital Red Queen framework with DEI, nodes share local optimal solutions at the end of each round to seed the next round's population. This creates cross-model adversarial pressure that drives robustness beyond intra-model self-play. Evaluated on the Core War domain, a competitive programming benchmark in which Redcode warrior programs battle inside a simulated machine, a four-node heterogeneous ensemble (GPT-5.4-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.2, and Claude Haiku 4.5) achieves 124 percent higher merged-archive QD-Score (45.90 vs. 20.46) and 28 percent higher coverage (80.6 percent vs. 63.0 percent of cells) than a single-node baseline at equal total LLM-call budget. The heterogeneous ensemble also outperforms an equally-budgeted homogeneous ensemble on QD-Score, coverage, and held-out solution generality across all four model families. These results provide the first empirical evidence that model diversity, not merely parallelism, is the key driver of gain in distributed LLM-based QD search.

</details>


### [179] [The Role of Causal Features in Strategic Classification for Robustness and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27163)
*Antonio Gois,Sophia Gunluk,Nir Rosenfeld,Nidhi Hegde,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Dhanya Sridhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了因果模型在策略分类中的作用，证明了因果分类在噪声有界时能实现最优分类误差，分析了OOD交叉熵风险的分解，并发现因果特征有助于对齐机构与用户的长期激励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略分类中，用户会根据分类器调整自身特征以增加效用，导致分布偏移。现有方法难以应对这种自适应引发的分布变化，作者试图引入因果模型来约束最坏情况下的分布外风险。

Method: 理论推导与实证验证相结合。通过因果分类分析噪声有界条件下的最优分类误差，分解OOD交叉熵风险为偏差项和特征使用不足项，并研究因果特征对长期激励对齐的影响。

Result: 因果分类在噪声有界时达到最优分类误差；OOD交叉熵风险可分解为偏差项和未使用全部特征导致的项；使用因果特征可以对齐机构与用户的长期激励，与以往工作指出的社会成本相反。

Conclusion: 因果模型在策略分类中具有理论优势，能够提供更鲁棒的分类性能，同时在长期激励对齐方面具有正面社会影响。

Abstract: In strategic classification, an institution (e.g., a bank) anticipates adaptation from users who change their features to increase utility in a classification task (e.g., loan repayment). Since a key challenge is the distribution shift induced by users, we turn to causal models, which have been shown to bound the worst-case out-of-distribution (OOD) risk, and establish several new results that link causality and strategic classification. First, we show that causal classification leads to optimal classification error after any sufficiently large adaptation, when the noise is bounded in a certain way. Second, when these assumptions do not hold, we show OOD cross-entropy risk of optimal classifiers decomposes into an OOD bias term and a term arising from not using all observable features, allowing us to understand when causal classifiers have an advantage. Finally, we show that the use of causal features can allow alignment of long-term incentives between institutions and users, contrasting with previous work that highlights social costs of such approaches. We validate our theory empirically on synthetic data, finding that our results predict behavior in practice.

</details>


### [180] [MobileMoE: Scaling On-Device Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27358)
*Yanbei Chen,Hanxian Huang,Ernie Chang,Jacob Szwejbka,Digant Desai,Zechun Liu,Vikas Chandra,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MobileMoE，一系列面向设备端部署的子十亿参数MoE语言模型（0.3-0.9B激活/1.3-5.3B总参数），通过制定设备端MoE缩放定律、采用适度稀疏+细粒度共享专家的最优架构、四阶段训练流程（预训练-中期训练-指令微调-量化感知训练），在14个基准测试中以2-4倍更少的推理FLOPs达到或超越领先密集模型，并在真实智能手机上实现1.8-3.8倍prefill加速和2.2-3.4倍decode加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构已在千亿参数大语言模型中成为事实标准，但其在子十亿规模、面向移动设备部署场景下的优势尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索MoE在设备端小模型中的潜力。

Method: 1）制定设备端MoE缩放定律，在内存和计算约束下联合优化MoE架构，发现"适度稀疏+细粒度共享专家"为最优配置；2）基于推导架构，采用四阶段训练流程（预训练、中期训练、指令微调、量化感知训练），全部使用开源数据集；3）在真实商用智能手机上实现首个高效MoE推理并完成全面设备端性能分析。

Result: 在14个基准测试中，MobileMoE以2-4倍更少的推理FLOPs达到或超越领先的密集设备端LLM；相比SOTA MoE模型OLMoE-1B-7B，以最多60%更少参数达到或超越其性能。在INT4权重内存相当的情况下，MobileMoE-S相比密集基线MobileLLM-Pro实现1.8-3.8倍prefill加速和2.2-3.4倍decode加速。

Conclusion: MobileMoE建立了设备端LLM的新Pareto前沿，证明了MoE架构在子十亿规模移动部署场景下的显著优势，并通过真实手机上的高效推理实现弥合了模型研究与移动部署之间的最后一公里。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the de facto architecture for hundred-billion-parameter language models, yet its advantages at sub-billion scales for on-device deployment remain largely unexplored. To close this gap, we present MobileMoE, a family of on-device MoE language models with sub-billion active parameters (0.3-0.9B active and 1.3-5.3B total) that establish a new Pareto frontier for on-device LLMs. We first formulate an on-device MoE scaling law that jointly optimizes MoE architecture under mobile memory and compute constraints, identifying an on-device sweet spot - moderate sparsity with fine-grained and shared experts - that is simultaneously memory and compute-optimal. Building on the derived architectures, we train MobileMoE with a four-stage recipe covering pre-training, mid-training, instruction fine-tuning, and quantization-aware training, all on open-source datasets. Across 14 benchmarks, MobileMoE matches or exceeds leading on-device dense LLMs with 2-4$\times$ fewer inference FLOPs, and matches or surpasses the state-of-the-art MoE OLMoE-1B-7B with up to 60% fewer parameters. To bridge the last mile to mobile deployment, we provide the first efficient MoE inference on commodity smartphones with comprehensive on-device profiling. At comparable INT4 weight memory, MobileMoE-S delivers $1.8$-$3.8\times$ faster prefill and $2.2$-$3.4\times$ faster decode than the dense baseline MobileLLM-Pro.

</details>


### [181] [Nonlinear Data Integration via Kernel Methods for Data Collaboration Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27219)
*Yamato Suetake,Yuta Kawakami,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非线性核集成（NKI）方法，通过核化线性核集成（LKI）实现非线性中间表示的对齐，并引入图正则化和中心化约束以纳入几何与目标变量信息，在图像分类任务中相比现有线性集成方法提升了分类精度并降低了重构风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据协作（DC）分析方法多依赖线性变换进行数据混淆与集成，面临重构风险高的问题；非线性降维虽能降低风险，但传统线性集成方法无法准确对齐非线性中间表示；且现有集成方法主要最小化各方差异，未显式纳入几何或目标变量信息用于下游分析。

Method: 首先形式化线性核集成（LKI）作为线性集成方法，然后通过核化得到非线性核集成（NKI）。NKI通过核岭回归和特征值问题求解全局最优解，并引入图正则化和中心化约束，使目标表示能够捕获几何和目标变量信息。

Result: 在图像分类任务上，NKI在非线性降维条件下显著提升了分类精度；引入目标变量感知的图正则化和中心化约束后性能进一步提升；降维方法的选择对分类精度和重构风险均有显著影响。

Conclusion: NKI有效克服了线性集成方法在非线性中间表示对齐上的局限性，通过纳入辅助信息进一步提升了下游任务性能，为隐私保护下的数据协作分析提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Collaborative analysis of decentralized confidential datasets is important, but direct sharing of original datasets is often restricted by privacy and institutional constraints. Data collaboration (DC) analysis transforms each dataset into privacy-preserving intermediate representations via party-specific obfuscation functions and integrates them into common collaboration representations using an anchor dataset. However, many existing DC analysis methods rely on linear transformations for data obfuscation and integration, which may increase reconstruction risk. Although nonlinear dimensionality reduction can mitigate this risk, conventional linear integration methods cannot accurately align intermediate representations produced by nonlinear transformations. Moreover, existing integration methods mainly minimize discrepancies among parties and do not explicitly incorporate geometric or target-variable information useful for downstream analysis. To overcome these limitations, we first formulate linear kernel integration (LKI) as a linear integration method and then kernelize it to obtain nonlinear kernel integration (NKI). NKI admits a globally optimal solution via kernel ridge regression and an eigenvalue problem. We also introduce graph regularization and a centering constraint so that the target representation can capture geometric and target-variable information useful for downstream analysis. Experiments on image classification tasks demonstrate that NKI improves classification accuracy over existing linear integration methods under nonlinear dimensionality reduction, with further gains from target-variable-aware graph regularization and centering. The results also show that dimensionality reduction choices substantially affect both classification accuracy and reconstruction risk.

</details>


### [182] [Explainable Comparison of Feature-Based and Deep Learning Models for TROPOMI Methane Plume Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27236)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Joannes D. Maasakkers,Berend J. Schuit,Ilse Aben*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了基于特征（SVC、随机森林、XGBoost）和基于图像（ResNet-18、ResNet-34）的模型用于甲烷羽流-伪影分类，发现图像模型在平衡和不平衡评估下表现更优，并结合SHAP可解释性分析为实际甲烷筛查工作流提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星检测到的甲烷排放羽流中存在大量由地形、反照率梯度、气溶胶等造成的检索伪影，需区分真实排放与伪影；传统基于专家设计特征的SVC方法受限于特征空间且丢失空间信息，需探索更优的分类方法。

Method: 对比基于特征的传统机器学习模型（SVC、随机森林、XGBoost）与基于图像的深度学习模型（ResNet-18、ResNet-34）在甲烷羽流-伪影分类任务上的表现，在平衡与不平衡评估设置下进行实验，并使用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: 图像模型相较于特征模型在甲烷羽流-伪影分类中表现更优，但需结合实际评估设置（平衡/不平衡）进行模型选择。

Conclusion: 为CAMS甲烷热点探索器等业务化甲烷筛查工作流中的模型选择提供了实用指导，强调了图像模型保留空间信息的重要性。

Abstract: Continuous and global detection of large methane emissions is a crucial step for global warming mitigation. Satellite observations, such as from S5P/TROPOMI, combined with plume detection algorithms, can play a key role in this effort. However, not all TROPOMI plume detections that look like methane emission plumes are the result of actual emissions. A significant part of the plume-like features in the data are retrieval artifacts. Such artifacts could be the result of variations in elevation or albedo gradients, high concentrations of aerosols, coastal lines, water bodies, etc. Previous work approached the problem of plume-artifact classification by means of a Support Vector Machine Classifier (SVC), trained on an extensive set of observation-based scalar features designed by domain experts. However, such an approach limits the information scope received by the algorithm to what is deemed to be important by the experts, breaks the spatial relationship between pixels, and loses information during the process of statistical aggregation. In this study, we compare feature-based (SVC, Random Forest, XGBoost) and image-based (ResNet-18, ResNet-34) models for methane plume-artifact classification under balanced and imbalanced evaluation settings. To interpret the results, we apply SHAP-based explainability to both model families. Our findings provide practical guidance for model selection in operational methane-screening workflows such as the CAMS Methane Hotspot Explorer.

</details>


### [183] [Symbolic Regression via Latent Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27245)
*Xieting Chu,Sriram Vishwanath,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent Equation Embedding (LEE)框架，通过迭代摊销推理和可微梯度优化，在函数意义潜空间中进行符号回归，在SRBench上相比19个基线方法取得2-10倍更简洁的表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号回归方法通过单次编码将观测直接映射到表达式，但这种摊销推理存在"摊销间隙"（amortization gap），即单次预测与真实后验分布之间存在差距。作者旨在通过迭代摊销推理来弥合这一间隙。

Method: LEE框架包含三个组件：(1) 编码器f_θ，将符号token和数值观测联合嵌入到潜向量z；(2) 表达式解码器g_expr，从z重建公式；(3) 评估解码器g_eval，从z预测函数值。推理时进行迭代精炼：将解码的表达式与观测重新编码，逐步改进潜估计。同时结合可微梯度下降（g_eval对z可微）与离散重新编码，形成混合迭代-梯度优化过程。

Result: 在SRBench上跨越三个噪声水平，对比19个基线方法（包括遗传规划、符号-神经混合体、预训练Transformer），LEE产生的表达式复杂度为8-11（基线的复杂度为20-90），即简洁2-10倍。在准确率-复杂度帕累托前沿的低复杂度区域取得进展，且随噪声增加性能退化优雅。

Conclusion: LEE通过迭代摊销推理有效弥合了神经符号回归中的摊销间隙，在保持竞争力的准确率的同时显著降低表达式复杂度，特别适合需要可解释性的应用场景。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) seeks closed-form mathematical expressions that fit observed data. Neural SR methods amortize the search by training an encoder to map observations directly to expressions in a single pass, but this amortized inference leaves a residual amortization gap between its one-shot prediction and the true posterior. We propose Latent Equation Embedding (LEE), a framework that closes this gap through iterative amortized inference in a functionally grounded latent space. LEE learns a shared latent space Z equipped with three components: an encoder f_theta that jointly embeds symbolic tokens and numerical observations into a single latent vector z; an expression decoder g_expr that reconstructs formulas from z; and an evaluation decoder g_eval that predicts function values from z, explicitly grounding the latent space in functional behavior. At inference, LEE performs iterative refinement by re-encoding decoded expressions jointly with observations, progressively improving the latent estimate. LEE uses the encoder itself as a learned inference optimizer: each re-encoding step implicitly computes the mismatch between the candidate and the data. Because g_eval is differentiable in z, we additionally interleave continuous gradient descent with discrete re-encoding, yielding a hybrid iterative and gradient refinement procedure. On SRBench across three noise levels, against 19 baselines spanning genetic programming, symbolic-neural hybrids, and pre-trained Transformers, LEE produces expressions 2--10x simpler than the strongest accuracy-oriented baselines, including Operon, GP-GOMEA, TPSR, RAG-SR, and GenSR, with complexity 8--11 versus 20--90. These results advance the low-complexity region of the accuracy-complexity Pareto frontier and show graceful degradation as noise increases.

</details>


### [184] [Kan Extension Transformers: A Categorical Unification of Attention, Diffusion, and Predict-Detach Self-Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27259)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kan扩展变换器（KETs），将Transformer层统一为加权结构化扩展算子框架，覆盖标准注意力、几何Transformer和KET高阶单纯复形情况，并引入预测分离机制作为有效的自条件化方法。实验表明，预测分离机制带来的提升大于邻域族变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer实现多样化但缺乏统一的理论框架，不同变体（标准注意力、几何Transformer等）之间的关系未被系统刻画，以及非因果结构暴露与未来信息泄露之间的权衡问题有待解决。

Method: 将Transformer层建模为加权结构化扩展算子，其中标准注意力对应单邻域情形，几何Transformer的入射混合对应稀疏边限制情形，KET对应高阶单纯复形情形。引入预测分离机制，将扩展算子作用于分离的预测载体而非教师强制隐状态，实现不泄露未来令牌的非因果结构暴露。

Result: 在Penn Treebank、WikiText-2和WikiText-103上验证12种不同Transformer实现。严格因果设置下，二次型KET在WikiText-2和WikiText-103上表现最强。但所有数据集上，预测分离机制带来的提升大于仅改变邻域族。

Conclusion: KET框架统一了多种Transformer架构，预测分离机制比架构选择更重要。

Abstract: We propose Kan Extension Transformers (KETs) as a unifying categorical framework for a diverse group of Transformer implementations. The core claim is that a Transformer layer can be viewed as a weighted structured extension operator: standard attention is the singleton-neighborhood case, Geometric Transformer style incidence mixing is a sparse edge-restricted case, and KET is the higher-order simplicial case. This lens also clarifies a bridge to diffusion-style completion. When the extension operator acts on detached predictive carriers instead of teacher-forced hidden states, it becomes a valid self-conditioning mechanism that exposes noncausal structure without leaking gold future tokens. We include a comprehensive experimental validation of 12 different Transformer implementations varying across strict-causal and predict-detach regimes on Penn Treebank, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. In the strict-causal setting, quadratic KET is the strongest model among the compared causal architectures on WikiText-2 and WikiText-103. Across all datasets, however, the largest gains come from the predict-detach regime rather than from changing the neighborhood family alone.

</details>


### [185] [Transfer Learning using 66 Diseases for Disease Forecasting Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27269)
*Lauren J Beesley,Alexander C Murph,Dave Osthus,Lauren A Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究了通过整合多种数据流和跨疾病迁移学习来提升传染病预测模型性能的方法，发现84.9%的时间序列和模型结构因额外数据流而改进，但数据质量至关重要，并贡献了一个公开的传染病数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统疾病预测模型通常依赖单一数据流，在历史数据短或有噪声时表现脆弱。近期研究表明整合多种报告系统或使用迁移学习可提升性能，但以往工作规模有限，需要更系统地评估多数据流和跨疾病学习的价值。

Method: 训练覆盖66种传染病和多个数据流的机器学习模型，对20种疾病数据流进行预测，系统评估不同数据流组合的效果，并构建公开数据库。

Result: 整合其他数据流在84.9%的时间序列和模型结构中提升了预测性能，但添加与目标数据流差异过大的数据可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 多数据流融合可显著提升传染病预测，但需注意数据质量与相关性；同时公开了一个可供研究社区使用的综合性传染病数据库。

Abstract: Disease forecasting models typically rely on a single data stream, making models brittle when histories are short or noisy. Recent top-performing models have shown that synthesizing multiple reporting systems for the same disease improves performance. Other recent work takes this idea a step further, using transfer learning to train a forecasting model for one disease using data from a different disease. We expand upon each of these approaches greatly, training machine learning models on data that span 66 infectious diseases and several data streams. We investigate the value of incorporating different data streams for forecasting 20 different disease data streams. We find that incorporating other data streams improves forecasting in the vast majority (84.9%) of time series and model structures considered. However, our work highlights that the quality of the added data matters, where adding data extremely different from the target data stream can sometimes degrade forecast performance. A major contribution of this work is in compiling a publicly-available database of data for use by the infectious disease forecasting community.

</details>


### [186] [Causal Risk Minimization for High-Dimensional Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27281)
*Nikita Dhawan,Arnav Paruthi,Andrew Kim,Lovedeep Gondara,Jekaterina Novikova,Chris J. Maddison*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在超高维处理空间（如文本）中进行因果推断的方法，通过将因果误差分解为一系列矩平衡误差，并设计优化目标来改善因果估计，同时支持将高维处理效果投影到低维属性上，无需针对每个属性单独训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果估计器假设所有可能的干预都是可观测的，但在干预空间极大（如所有文本字符串）时这一假设不成立，需要开发能够处理高维处理空间的因果推断方法。

Method: 将因果推断重新表述为学习问题，证明因果误差可分解为一系列递增阶数的矩平衡误差，设计直接改善因果估计的目标函数；同时提出将高维处理效果投影到低维处理属性的方法。

Result: 在高维连续、离散和文本处理（基于Amazon Reviews的半合成数据集）的实验中，高阶平衡误差优化带来了收益，投影因果估计与属性专用估计器的性能相当。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效处理高维处理空间的因果推断，投影方法允许多个因果问题用一个模型回答，无需额外训练。

Abstract: Predicting the effect of interventions with many possible variations, e.g., therapeutic content that affects mental health outcomes or an earnings call transcript that drives movement in share price, is useful across several domains. However, classical causal estimators tend to assume that all possible interventions are observed, which is infeasible when interventions vary widely, for instance, in the space of all text strings. We adapt a well-known approach of recasting causal inference as a learning problem, to address high-dimensional treatment spaces. Specifically, under standard assumptions like no unobserved confounding, we show that causal error decomposes into a series of moment-balancing errors of increasing order, and design objectives that directly improve causal estimation. We also show how to project the effect of a high-dimensional treatment onto lower-dimensional treatment attributes, which allows a single model to answer several causal questions without additional attribute-specific training. We empirically evaluate our estimators in settings with high-dimensional continuous, discrete, and text treatments, the last of which used a semi-synthetic dataset of Amazon Reviews.
